# safe-pdf progress ledger

Maintained by `/next-wave`. The orchestrator is the only writer of this file.
Parallel limit: 4
Usage gate: 95%
Design document: Plan.md

## How to read this file
* Status: `todo` · `doing (date)` · `done (date)` · `blocked (why)` · `split (into …)`.
* Needs: task IDs that must be `done` before this one starts.
* Owns: the files or directories this task may create or modify. Tasks whose Owns
  overlap never run in the same wave unless both are `wt`.
* Par: `new` (creates only new files; shared checkout; single-file checks only) ·
  `wt` (edits existing files; own git worktree; builds and tests there) ·
  `solo` (the orchestrator does it alone).
* Completed phases (0–8) keep only their exit lines here; their task tables are
  archived in [docs/progress-archive.md](docs/progress-archive.md). Later phases are
  deliberately coarse; each task is split (size check) when its phase becomes current.

## Current wave
Wave 4 (phase 13, PR #25), started 2026-11-28: 13.4b (wt) still running; 13.24, 13.27, 13.28 integrated.

## Phase 2 — File structure: xref, trailer, revisions ★
Plan.md §6 Phase 2, §5.2, §5.4. Exit: **M2**: 99% of corpus opens (strict or repaired); Bogk-style cyclic files all rejected in bounded time; first machine-checked proof that PDF xref resolution terminates on arbitrary input.
Tasks archived in [docs/progress-archive.md](docs/progress-archive.md).
Exit criterion checked: [x] (2026-11-19 on the wave-15 commit: dashboard 74/74 open (100%, all strict), 0 regressions; Bogk-style `/Prev` cycles and self-containing object streams fail `openFile` with `Error.cycle` in < 0.01 ms (`openFile_cycle`, `loadChain_cycle_rejected`, `loadChain_endless_cycle`; 141+ mutations pass); `/Length` cycles fail at the object (`cycle@225`, `resolveLength_container_cycle`); termination `Spec.XrefTerm.loadChain_terminates` (no `partial`/fuel; measure `bytes.size - visited.length`) with work bound `loadChainCounted_calls_le`; axioms OK (248))

## Phase 3 — Filters
Plan.md §6 Phase 3. Exit: **M3**: decompression-bomb suite (nested streams, 10⁶:1 ratios) all rejected within limits.
Tasks archived in [docs/progress-archive.md](docs/progress-archive.md).
Exit criterion checked: [x] (2026-11-19: `SAFEPDF_BOMBS_DEFAULT=1 lake exe tests` — 37 bomb cases at the 1 MiB limit (163 ms total) and 37 at the default 256 MiB limit (27.1 s total): every bomb rejected with `limitExceeded`, slowest call 2.7 s; nested Flate ×3 at 4.8·10⁶:1, Flate→Flate 6·10⁵:1, LZW KwKwK, RunLength, ASCII85, 8-filter chains, object-stream and xref-stream bombs; near-limit controls pass. Supporting proofs: `decodeStream_size_le`, `produced_le_maxFilters`, `flateDecodeW_steps_le`, `lzwDecode_size_le`, `decodeAllB_total`, `loadChainB_cost`)

Tasks archived in [docs/progress-archive.md](docs/progress-archive.md).
Exit criterion checked: [x] (2026-11-20 on 7569011, local corpus of 81 files / 2,450 pages, MuPDF 1.25.1: `python3 -I Tests/differential/run.py --comparator text-mupdf --comparator positions` — text similarity vs MuPDF **0.9949** over 2,436 pages (1,462 exact; target ≥ 0.98); glyph positions **5,720,507 / 5,720,507 within** max(1 pt, 0.1 × size), max 0.287 pt, median 0.000; 14 pages of arxiv-astro-ph_9805201 excluded as a documented MuPDF defect (Type 3 digit glyphs `/1`..`/9` → U+0001..U+0009 in MuPDF; pdftotext and the rendered page agree with us; signature-checked in `Tests/differential/known-reference-defects.json`; raw numbers with `--no-known`). pdfium not available in the sandbox; MuPDF is the reference used.)

## Phase 7 — Rasterizer
Plan.md §6 Phase 7. Exit: **M7**: ≥ 95% of corpus pages within perceptual threshold; median page < 100 ms compiled.
Tasks archived in [docs/progress-archive.md](docs/progress-archive.md).
Exit criterion checked: [x] (2026-11-20 on b5da79e, all 81 corpus files / 2,450 pages, MuPDF 1.25.1 at 72 dpi: `python3 -I Tests/differential/run.py --comparator pixels` — **2,435 / 2,450 pages within (99.39%)** (≤ 1% pixels differing, tolerance 32, 3×3), 11 identical, 0 failed; compiled page time **median 64.2 ms**, p95 247.9 ms, max 2.58 s (MuPDF median 2 ms). Earlier measurements: 88.08% before substitute fonts (7.7), 97.27% before CCITT (7.8), 99.39% on d575fd9. 7.6d moved to Tier 2. Substitute fonts need `fonts-urw-base35` (or TeX Gyre) installed; without them non-embedded text is unpainted.)

## Phase 10 — Viewer polish
Plan.md §6 Phase 10 (bib-lean reader mode descoped 2026-11-23 at the human's request — implemented on the bib-lean side; tasks 10.1–10.4 below are archived). Exit: **M10**: the polish tasks 10.5–10.10 and 10.18 land (smoke-tested).

## Phase 11 — Native macOS shell (Tk stays on Linux)

## Phase 12 — Hardening (independent review; docs/safety.md §5)
Plan.md §6 Phase 12 (added 2026-11-23 at the human's request). Exit: **M12**: the review's priority-1/2/3 acceptance criteria hold — a growing input reads only through the cap; a strict-only open records zero repair attempts; a platform test shows the worker cannot open unrelated files, reach the network or see the token, and a killed worker leaves the GUI able to open the next file; an interleaved zoom/search/thumbnail CI scenario stays within published memory ceilings.

## Phase 13 — Hardening II (the binary matches the claims)
Plan.md §6 Phase 13 (added 2026-11-25 at the human's request). Exit: **M13**: (1) time — the scaling suite passes on every probe (time at 2n ≤ 3 × time at n), the long-structure adversarial files finish or end in `limitExceeded` within their recorded budgets in the library and the inspector, the long-input fuzz run reports no case over its linear budget; (2) premises — the environment scan of the strict modules reports only the three axioms and no `partial`, `unsafe`, `@[extern]` or `@[implemented_by]` beyond compiler-generated ones, and the guard fails on each planted violation; (3) toolchain — tests, fuzz, adversarial and the pinned corpus pass on the assertions build, the C test binaries pass under sanitizers; (4) boundary — the scan shows the GUI roots of both shells do not reach the parser, interpreter, rasterizer or writer beyond the exceptions in Decisions; a stalled open or save ends at the deadline with a message, the window stays responsive and the next file opens; (5) speed — corpus page-time median and p95 no worse than the phase-start baseline on the same machine, no bignum constant rebuilt per sample or per point; (6) statements — `docs/safety.md`, the comparison tables and Plan.md §4 say what (1)–(4) establish.
Measurements quoted in the rows: 2026-11-25, Linux sandbox (aarch64, 8 cores), `inspect` built at 031d779, probes pinned to six cores at low priority; every module they concern (`Core/Content`, `Core/ObjParse.lean`, `Core/Raster`, `Core/Object.lean`, `scripts/hygiene.py`, `scripts/fuzz.sh`, `Tests/Fuzz`) is byte-identical on `main` at 430203d, which has not been rebuilt and re-measured yet — the first wave does. Order: 13.1, 13.2, 13.8, 13.17 first (independent, small); 13.4 and 13.12 start with a Decisions entry; tracks: time 13.1–13.7, premises 13.8–13.9, toolchain 13.10–13.11, boundary 13.12–13.16, speed 13.17–13.20, statements 13.21.
| ID | Task | Needs | Owns | Par | Status |

| 13.1 | **Path building is linear again** (regression from 9.r11a, `db41c69`). `Run.stepR` reads `m.oc` after `localStep env fr m s op`, so the machine stays shared across the call, `Interp.exec` hands a shared `Core` to `simple`, and every `addSegs` push copies the whole path array (stack samples: `lean_copy_expand_array` under `addSegs`, the frees under `stepR`). Fix: bind `m.oc` before the call. Checked on a scratch copy at 031d779: the bound version is `rfl`-equal to today's `stepR` and its IR no longer has `inc` of the machine before `localStep`. Re-check the proofs that unfold `stepR`; apply the same IR check to `gsR`, the `Do` branch and `localStep`'s branches; add the instance to `docs/proof-patterns.md`. ## Phase 14 — Signature verification
Plan.md §6 Phase 14 (added 2026-11-26 at the human's request, full scope; verification only, no signing). Exit: **M14**: (1) on the signed corpus every verdict agrees with `pdfsig`/OpenSSL (recorded trust-set differences aside); (2) every adversarial file (wrapping, shadow, tampering, ByteRange and certificate tricks) is flagged invalid or changed-after-signing with the right reason, within its budget; (3) the coverage theorem and the DER equivalence are axiom-clean and the verify primitives pass their vector suites; (4) both shells show the verdicts with the honest statuses, including "revocation not checked"; (5) the hygiene and environment scans stay clean.
Order: 14.1, 14.2, 14.3, 14.4 and 14.7 are independent starts; 14.11 is a solo Decisions entry before the worker/UI tasks; everything feeds 14.8.

## Gotchas (append-only; read before every wave, pasted into every brief)
* `qpdf`, `mutool`, `pdftotext` are not installed on the dev machine (2026-11-18); differential scripts must skip cleanly when absent.
* Installed Lean toolchains: v4.31.0, v4.35.0, v4.36.0 (elan 4.2.4).
* Lake layout (0.1): exes `inspect` (root `SafePdf.Shell.Main`), `tests` (`Tests.Main`), `fuzz` (`Tests.Fuzz.Main`); `lake build` builds lib `SafePdf` + `inspect`; tests/fuzz need `lake build tests fuzz`. Shared registries: `SafePdf.lean` (umbrella imports), `Tests/Main.lean` (`suites` list), `lakefile.toml`.
* Single-file check `lake env lean <file>` (0.2) takes ~0.2 s and only sees modules already built in the main checkout; new files importing other unbuilt new files cannot be checked this way.
* Worktree subagents may be branched from `main`, not the phase branch: briefs must say to check `git log` first and `git reset --hard <phase-branch>` if the branch is empty (0.6).
* Forbidden-construct greps that don't strip comments trip on Core module docs naming `partial`/`sorry`; `scripts/check-hygiene.sh` strips comments/strings, so use it as the authority (0.3, 0.6).
* `scripts/check-hygiene.sh` needs `lake build` first (the axioms step imports built modules); options `--root DIR`, `--no-axioms`. Every phase adds `#print axioms` lines for its top-level theorems to `scripts/axioms.lean` (0.3).
* `!` rule in the hygiene guard is a broad `ident!` match in Core/Spec/Repair; an identifier legitimately ending in `!` needs an allowlist entry in `scripts/hygiene.py` (0.3).
* ByteArray in v4.36: `emptyWithCapacity`, `extract`, `++`, `foldl`, `push`, `bytes[i]?` work; set a byte with no `!`/proof via `⟨b.data.modify i f⟩` (no-op out of range) (0.6).
* Timing a pure call in IO: `match` on its result before re-reading `IO.monoNanosNow`, else the computation may float past the clock read (0.6).
* `SafePdf.Core.Parse`: `parseWith (limits) (bytes)` / `parse`; `Document` is a placeholder (`headerOffset`, `version`, `byteLength`) to be replaced in Phase 1/2 (0.6).
* Fuzz driver reads seeds from `Tests/adversarial` and `corpus` by default, so `scripts/fuzz.sh` results (input-hash) change as the corpus grows; determinism holds for a fixed seed set (0.6).
* Corpus: `python3 -I scripts/corpus/fetch.py` (≈4 min first run, 74 arXiv PDFs, 184 MB, into git-ignored `corpus/files/`); `--add URL`, `--summary`, `--reindex`. Unversioned arXiv URLs serve the latest version — use `…/pdf/<id>vN` for new entries (0.8).
* arXiv `/Producer` reflects arXiv's rebuild (pdfTeX recent, Ghostscript/dvips pre-2012, pikepdf on 2025+ IDs); read Producer via the trailer's `/Info`, not the last `/Producer` in the file (embedded figures) (0.8).
* Corpus gaps (0.8): no LuaTeX/XeTeX/LibreOffice document producers, no scanned or publisher PDFs, no SafeDocs/PDF Association samples (license/URL unverified).
* Adversarial files (0.9): `python3 -I Tests/adversarial/generate.py [--check]` is deterministic; never open them in a viewer. Not yet consumed by any Lean test (that is 2.7); Bogk mutations not generated yet.

* Lake v4.36 only builds modules that belong to a `lean_lib`; an exe's local imports outside any lib fail with "object file … does not exist". Lib `Tests` now uses `globs = ["Tests.+"]` (0.5).
* Golden tests: `Tests/golden/<suite>/<input>` + `<input>.expected`; renderers registered in `Tests/Golden.lean` (`renderers`); regenerate with `SAFEPDF_GOLDEN_UPDATE=1 lake exe tests` then review `git diff Tests/golden` (0.5).
* Inspector (0.10): `inspect [--json] [--view NAME] [--list-views] <file>...`; views are `SafePdf/Shell/Views/<Name>.lean` modules registered in `views` in `SafePdf/Shell/Inspect.lean`; shared types in `Views/Common.lean` (`Config`, `Input`, `View`, `jsonString`/`jsonObject`). A trailing bare word is a view only if it's not an existing path — prefer `--view`. Exit: 0 ok, 1 parse error, 2 usage/IO.
* Don't import `Lean` (e.g. `Lean.Json`) in Shell/Core: it links the compiler library into the exe; JSON is hand-rolled in `Views/Common.lean` (0.10).
* v4.36: `String.mk` deprecated → `String.ofList`; no `List.forM'` → `forM` (0.10).
* Differential runner (0.7): `python3 -I Tests/differential/run.py [--comparator N] [--files 'GLOB'] [--limit N] [--json OUT]`; comparators skip on missing tool; non-`size` comparators skip until the inspector has `objects`/`pages`/`text` views — update its unknown-view heuristic to use inspector exit code 2 (0.7, 0.10).
* CI (0.4): `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, actions pinned by SHA (checkout v4.2.2, cache v4.2.0), `elan-init.sh` at elan v4.4.0 commit (elan binary itself not hash-pinned); `.lake` cache key includes all `*.lean` hashes — bump the `lean-` prefix on stale-cache trouble.
* Subagent Bash in worktrees: complex compound heredoc commands may be refused by the permission check; tell subagents to use the Write tool for new files (0.10).
* Object model (1.1): `PdfObject` = direct objects only; streams only in `IndirectBody.stream` of `IndirectObject {num, gen, body}`. Strings/names are decoded `List UInt8`; stream data `ByteArray`. Reals are `Decimal {mantissa, scale}`, canonical via `Decimal.normalize`; printer must print scale-0 reals with a `.` (e.g. `5.0`). `Dict := List (Name × PdfObject)`, `Dict.lookup` first match, `Dict.get?` treats null as absent. State round-trip over `PdfObject.Canonical` (decidable `isCanonical`).
* v4.36: `deriving DecidableEq` fails on nested inductives (`List T` fields) — hand-write from a mutual Boolean `beq` + soundness/reflexivity proofs (as in `Core/Object.lean`). `deriving BEq`/`Repr` work. `ByteArray` has `DecidableEq` but no `Repr`. Mutual structural recursion through `List`/`List (A × T)` needs no `termination_by` (1.1).
* v4.36: `decide` gets stuck on `String.toUTF8` — use `#guard` or literal byte lists; `if_false` deprecated → `ite_false`; `simp [f]` in case branches may leave `x ≤ max x y` → `Nat.le_max_left` (1.1).
* Dashboard (0.11): `python3 -I scripts/corpus/dashboard.py [--compare docs/dashboard.json [--write]]`; compare re-runs the inspector and exits 1 on regressions; `docs/dashboard.json` changes every run (timings/date), so use `--compare`, not diff. Regenerate `docs/dashboard.md` at each milestone.
* Lexer contract (wave 6): `SafePdf/Core/Token.lean` (orchestrator-owned) fixes `Token`, `LexError {offset, kind}` and the `next` signatures; Spec `Spec.Lex.next : List UInt8 → Except LexError (Option (Token × List UInt8))` (offsets relative), Core `Core.Lex.next : ByteArray → Nat → …(Token × Nat)` (offsets absolute). Name errors: leftmost problem decides the kind. A randomized differential check (61,500 input/position pairs, 0 disagreements) ran at integration; 1.6 should keep it as a test.
* Spec lexer structure (1.2): `next l = nextAt (l.length - (skipWs l).length) (skipWs l)`; helpers `skipWs/skipComment` (mutual structural), `regularRun`, `lexNumber` (decides the whole run after the fact), `litBody` (well-founded, `termination_by l.length`, depth counts extra opens from 0), `hexBody` + `packNibbles`, `decodeName`. Spec `lexAll` offsets are relative to its input.
* Core lexer structure (1.3): `next bytes pos = tokenAt bytes (skipWs bytes pos false)`; scanners `readRun`, `litStr` (depth from 1, ends on `)` at depth ≤ 1, escapes via `escapeAt`/`octalAt`), `hexStr` (pending high nibble), `nameRun`; all accumulators are reversed lists; `next_pos` gives progress. `lexAll` ≈ 1.9 MB/s interpreted (#eval).
* v4.36 proofs: nested `match bs with | c :: cs => match cs …` breaks structural recursion → `termination_by` + named matches (`match h : …`, name `_h` if unused); `if_pos/if_neg/dif_pos/dif_neg` deprecated (use `simp`, `dite_eq_left/right`); `split at h` fails behind `let` — pass the scrutinee as a function parameter; `getElem?_neg` works for `ByteArray`; `fun_induction` IH names unreliable → `rename_i` (1.2, 1.3).
* **C5 for the lexer is proved** (1.6): `SafePdf.Spec.LexEquiv.next_eq` (no hypothesis on `pos`) and `lexAll_eq` (plain equality). Proof pattern (seed for 1.9): one `restAt` bridge (`restAt_cons`, `restAt_nil`, `length_restAt`), one lemma per scanner via `fun_induction` with generalized accumulators (`acc.reverse ++ ·`, position = `size - rest.length`), unfold well-founded spec functions one step with `conv => lhs; rw [f.eq_def]`, `generalize` before dependent matches, `decide` over all 256 bytes for byte-class facts, dispatch by `by_cases` on first byte, suffix lemmas for iteration.
* Object grammar spec (1.4): `Spec.Object.Parses/ParsesItems/ParsesEntries` (fuel, input, object, rest) + executable `parse/parseItems/parseEntries` (well-founded on `(ts.length, 0|1)`); `parse_eq_ok_iff`, `parse_isError_iff`, `Parses.unique` (S6), `Parses.depth_le`, `Parses.length_lt`, `Parses.canonical` (needs canonical real tokens + NUL-free name tokens) — all proved. `rw [parseItems] at h` leaves a side goal → `rotate_left`.
* Object parser (1.5): `Core.ObjParse.parseObj fuel bytes pos` → `parseObjTok` (mutual with `parseArray`/`parseDict`, reversed acc, measure `(fuel, size - pos)`); loops guard recursion with `if _hr : pos < r` (else branch unreachable — 1.8 discharges it via `parseObjTok_pos`); ref lookahead `parseIntTail` re-lexes (≤3 lexes per int); dict duplicate check `hasKey` is O(n²) (10k keys: 4 s interpreted). `parseIndirect` handles `n g obj … endobj` and streams with direct `/Length`; indirect `/Length` → `Error.unsupported`.
* **For 1.8**: the core parser lexes lazily and never sees lexer errors after the object's last token (`1 0 R )` succeeds), and a lexer error inside the ref lookahead (`5 )`) is an error. So the equivalence must be stated against the spec run on the tokens actually lexed (a lazily produced token prefix), not on `Spec.Lex.lexAll` of the whole input; compare successes exactly and failures only as "both fail".
* Hygiene guard: `s!`/`m!`/`f!` interpolation macros are exempt from the `ident!` rule (fixed at wave 7 integration); `get!`/`head!` etc. still flagged.
* v4.36: `conv_lhs`, `swap`, `on_goal` are Mathlib-only — use `conv => lhs; …`, `case neg => …`, `rotate_left`; `show` is a keyword (can't name a helper `show`); `Except` has no `BEq` (compare via `Sum` in tests); no `List.nodup_reverse` (use `List.pairwise_reverse`); `simp` equation lemmas don't fire on functions with overlapping patterns → `rw [f.eq_def]`; mutual theorem blocks need every member recursive (else "unused termination_by"); `↓reduceDIte`/`↓reduceIte` in `simp only` reduce termination guards (1.4–1.6).
* **M1 round trip proved** (wave 8): `SafePdf.Spec.RoundTripCore.parseObject_print : o.Canonical → RefsInRange o → o.depth ≤ limits.maxObjectDepth → ∃ r, Core.ObjParse.parseObject limits (print o) 0 = .ok (o, r)` — composes 1.7 (`lexAll_print`, `parse_printTokens`) with 1.8 (`parseObj_of_spec_full`). Exact end position (`r = (print o).size`) would need `dropToks (printTokens o).length (printObj o) = some []` + `ObjEquiv.parseObj_of_spec_full_size` — optional strengthening.
* Printer (1.7): `printObj o = render (printTokens o)`, tokens joined by one SP, no leading/trailing whitespace; ints via own `natDigits`; reals always with `.` (`5.`, `-3.5`, `0.005`); strings always hex `<…>` uppercase; names keep 0x21–0x7E non-delimiter non-`#`, else `#XX`. `RefsInRange` (n ≥ 1, g ≤ 65535) lives in `Core/Print.lean` — `Canonical` does not cover ref ranges.
* Parser equivalence (1.8): `ObjEquiv.LexFrom bytes p ps` (lazy token stream with end positions), `parseObj_ok_iff`/`parseObj_error_iff` (given `LexFrom`), `_lexAll` variants (whole remainder lexes), `parseObj_of_spec_full(_size)`, `dropToks`. Proof: one induction on token count replaces the mutual well-founded recursion; unfolding lemmas for the impl loops (`parseArray_none/_some`, `parseDict_*`) via `rw [f]; split`. `Core.ObjParse.keywordObj`/`kwR` are `rfl`-equal to the spec's.
* Golden suites (1.10): `objects` (24) and `indirect` (9) via test-only renderer `Tests/ObjRender.lean`; `Core/Parse.lean` gains `parseObjectAt`/`parseIndirectAt`. Fuzz driver runs 6 entry points per iteration with per-entry summaries; slowest-call timings on a separate `fuzz: timing` line (not deterministic). 220k iterations: 0 slow, 0 crashes, slowest 6 ms.
* `decode`/`unfold` pitfalls (1.7): `unfold f` also rewrites inside `flatMap` and breaks IHs — use small `rw` lemmas; `rw [← h]` with `h : f x = x` rewrites every occurrence; ambiguous dotted constructors (`.int`) → write `PdfObject.int` (1.8).
* `docs/proof-patterns.md` (1.9) documents the spec/impl/equivalence pattern with cited snippets; its "TODO (after 1.7/1.8)" section is still to be filled (fold into 1.12 or later doc pass).
* Inspector `objects` view (1.12): `inspect --view objects [--json] [--all] FILE` — Shell-level linear scan for `int int obj`, `parseIndirectAt` each, last (num, gen) wins unless `--all`. JSON lines per the 1.11 contract + `{"summary": …}`; K = successfully parsed objects. Exit code still comes from the header check (header-less inputs exit 1 with valid output). ~1 MB in 30 ms.
* qpdf comparator (1.11): `Tests/differential/objects.py` (`--ours F --qpdf F` offline mode; fixtures in `Tests/differential/fixtures/`), plugged into `run.py` as `objects`. Uses `qpdf --json=2 --json-key=qpdf --decode-level=none` — **needs qpdf ≥ 11 (JSON v2)**: ubuntu-22.04 has 10.6, use ubuntu-24.04. qpdf exit 3 (warnings) = success; other failures → skip (watch the skipped count). Normalizes strings/names to bytes, numbers to Decimal (int vs real = warning), drops `/Length` and null dict entries, ignores trailer. Never run against real qpdf yet; unverified assumptions listed in its docstring.
* Classic xref (2.1): `Core.Xref` — `XrefEntry` (free/inUse/compressed), `XrefSection {entries : List (Nat × XrefEntry), sorted}` (strictly ascending ⇒ one entry per object, merge-friendly for 2.2/2.5), `Trailer.ofDict` (`size`, `prev?`, `root?`, `xrefStm?`, `encrypted`; null = absent), `findStartxref` (last `startxref` in final 1024 bytes, strict `%%EOF`), `parseXrefTable`; `entryAt_eq` bridges the 20-byte reader to `entrySpec`. Not checked here: object numbers vs `/Size`, `/Prev`/`/XRefStm` offsets (2.2 must range-check before following). No acceptance-completeness theorem.
* v4.36: `simp` can't rewrite a dependent `match hh : f x` after `rw [f]` — use the dead-guard trick (proof-patterns §3.2); `simp only [f]` unfolds recursively, `rw [f]` unfolds one step; core has `List.mergeSort`, `mem_mergeSort`, `length_mergeSort` (2.1).
* **xref `/Prev` termination proved** (2.2): `Core.Xref.Chain.walk` carries a Nodup visited list of offsets < size, measure `bytes.size - visited.length`, heart lemma `length_lt_of_fresh` (via core `List.Nodup.length_le_of_subset` into `List.range n`). `Spec.XrefTerm.loadChain_cycle_rejected` covers cycles of every length (error kind not pinned: a >4096-revision tail hits the limit first); `loadChainCounted_calls_le` (parses ≤ size and ≤ 4096); `mergeChain_lookup` (newest revision wins). Adversarial `xref-prev-self`/`xref-prev-cycle` rejected with "xref /Prev cycle" (#guard, byte-exact embeds). `/XRefStm` not followed; `/Prev` to an xref stream errors (2.4).
* Indirect `/Length` (2.3): `Core.Xref.Length.resolveLengthAux` (count × result, structural on fuel, visiting check first), `parseIndirectResolved lookup limits bytes fuel pos` (keeps the original dict, data = resolved length); `length-self-ref.pdf` → "cyclic /Length"; `length-mutual.pdf` only "unsupported" until xref streams (2.4) — replace its hand-built lookup then. Duplicates `parseHeader`/`parseIndirectBody`/`tokenAt_pos` from ObjParse/Lex (task 2.10).
* Repair (2.6): `Repair.scanObjects` byte-level `headerAt` at boundaries + `parseIndirect` jump-past, newest wins (`mergeSort keyLe` + `keepAsc`), cap `maxObjects`; `scanSection` builds a sorted `XrefSection` with `(0, free 0 65535)`; `repair` errors only on no objects / no root. Failure budget 256: after it, headers are recorded but not parsed (bounds attempts but not bytes read per attempt; adversarial 1 MB of `1 0 obj (` = 60 s interpreted). Indirect-/Length streams fail fast and are not jumped over (fake headers inside can be reported) — fix in 2.10. Xref-stream trailers aren't candidates (loses `/Encrypt`) until 2.4.
* CI job `differential-objects` (1.13) on ubuntu-24.04 with apt qpdf (asserts ≥ 11), corpus cached on manifest hash, debug step printing qpdf/inspector heads, `run.py --min-compared 20`; artifact via upload-artifact v4.6.2 `ea165f8d65b6e75b540449e92b4886f43607fa02`. **First run not yet seen** — the next wave must read it, fix comparator assumptions, triage into `docs/deviations-from-spec.md`, then check M1.
* Pushes touching `.github/workflows/` are rejected (token without `workflow` scope, wave 29): CI changes go into a patch under `docs/` + a Blocked entry; never put workflow edits in a wave commit.
* Permission: a subagent brief asking to push/delete temporary remote branches to iterate on CI was refused by the permission classifier (wave 10). Keep subagents local; the orchestrator pushes only phase branches and reads CI in the next wave.
* v4.36: no `List.Nodup.subperm` (use `List.Nodup.length_le_of_subset`); `Option.noConfusion` on `none = some _` fails → `cases`; `<+` not available unqualified → `List.Sublist`; `(d, ·)` inside `.map` doesn't parse → `fun r => (d, r)`; `parseStreamBody` has a `let` → `dsimp only at h` before `split` (2.2, 2.3, 2.6).
* **First qpdf comparison run** (CI run 44402582274, wave 11): 74 files, 73 with objects compared; 55,870 / 55,902 objects agree; 9,912 qpdf-only (object streams); 287 our errors (not yet triaged — mostly indirect `/Length` streams, expected until the resolved parser is wired into the view); 32 disagreements, all `/Group N 0 R` → free object N ⇒ D-001 `dangling-ref-null` in `docs/deviations-from-spec.md` (comparator now warns instead); 1 error: inspector timeout on arxiv-2307.09288 (13.7 MB) → task 1.14.
* Flate (3.1): `Core.Filter.Flate` — output buffer `Out limit := {b : ByteArray // b.size ≤ limit}` makes the bound structural; puff-style canonical Huffman (`counts`/`symbols`, bit-by-bit `decodeGo`, fuel 15); `inflate`, `inflateAt` (returns bit pos), `zlibDecode … (checkAdler := true)`, `flateDecode limits`; `set_option maxRecDepth 4000 in` needed for `codes`. Interpreted speed: ~0.34 MB/s Huffman-heavy, ~2.9 MB/s back-ref-heavy, ~5 MB/s stored — compiled speed unmeasured (Plan §8 open question). Work-bound theorem (c) NOT proved (follow-up). 40 KB hex vectors live in the Core file (move to Tests later). Use zlib's 32-code fixed distance table (puff's 30 is incomplete and `build` rejects it).
* Predictors (3.2): `Core.Filter.Predictor` — `Params.ofDict` (Table 10 defaults, `/Colors` ≤ 32, `/Columns` ≤ 2^24), shared `unfilterByte` for spec and impl, `decodePng_size` (exact length law), `decode_size_le` (output ≤ input, so no new limit); partial rows are strict errors; TIFF 2 for bpc 8/16 only.
* Adversarial suite (2.7): `Tests/Adversarial.lean` (suite `adversarial`) with an expectations table + 141 deterministic mutations of the cyclic files; `SAFEPDF_ADV_VERBOSE=1` prints every result; all calls < 5 ms compiled. Tighten `length-mutual`/`objstm-*` rows to real cycle errors when 2.4 lands.
* Limits (2.9): `Limits.maxRevisions := 4096` (→ `limitExceeded .revisions`), `Limits.maxRepairFailures := 256`; theorems restated over the fields.
* v4.36 (wave 11): `Subtype.property _` fails higher-order unification on subtype-valued results — wrap in an `Except` helper and project; a `let i := …` inside a termination-checked body yields copies `omega` treats as unrelated — inline it; `bs[k]? = bs.data[k]?` for ByteArray isn't closed by `simp` — use `getElem?_pos`/`getElem?_neg`; `ite_eq_left_of_eq_true` replaces deprecated `if_pos`.
* Usage gate: the loop checks `mcp__ccd_session_mgmt__get_usage` before each wave (gate 95%); at 100% of the 5-hour window it waits for the reset (wave 11 integration was deferred across one reset).
* Inspector timeout root cause (1.14): after a failed stream parse the Shell scan lexed binary stream data; each unbalanced `(` made `Core.Lex.litStr` run to EOF ⇒ O(#`(` × size). Fix (Shell only): resume after the next `endstream` for failed streams, and re-parse indirect-`/Length` streams via `Core.Xref.Length.parseIndirectResolved` with a lookup from scanned headers. Corpus: max 0.12 s/file (was > 20 s), 67,474 objects (was 54,678), 1 error (`arxiv-2512.00001` obj 37 duplicate key — genuine). **Residual risk:** any caller that retries the lexer byte-by-byte over garbage is quadratic (Core is linear per call; repair is capped by `maxRepairFailures`).
* Xref streams (2.4a): `Core.Xref.Stream.parseXrefStream`; `Chain.parseSection` dispatches table vs stream; `walk` recurses through a non-recursive `step` returning a proof-carrying `StepOk` record (fresh, distinct, in-bounds offsets) — `fun_induction walk` stays clean. Hybrid `/XRefStm` parsed in the same step (visited, counts toward `maxRevisions`; cycle → "xref /XRefStm cycle"); lookup order table → its XRefStm → older. Unknown entry types → `free 0 0` (§7.5.8.3). `PrevStep`/`Terminal` now mean "`parseSection` ok" (`PrevStep.of_table` shows the old relation is contained — cycle theorem strengthened). All 74 corpus files `loadChain` in 2–27 ms (66 single table, 4 two tables, 2 pure xref streams, 2 hybrid).
* Object streams (2.4b): `Core.Xref.ObjStm` — `ObjStm` structure carries proof fields (sorted offsets, bounds, nums ≥ 1, distinct via mergeSort-neighbour check, not O(n²)); `fetchCompressed lookup limits bytes fuel num` does ≤ 1 container parse + ≤ 1 decode (`fetchCompressed_work`), container must be `inUse … 0` (nested/self → error; `fetchCompressed_rank` is the Plan §5.2 rank argument). Strict: extra header tokens and non-whitespace after the last object are errors (may cost corpus files — watch the qpdf comparison).
* Adversarial expectations updated at wave 12 integration: `length-mutual.pdf` `loadChain` now `.ok` (its attack is the `/Length` cycle at resolution time, still asserted as an error); `objstm-self-container.pdf` now asserts "own object stream". A subagent may not edit `Tests/` expectations; the orchestrator does it at integration with a comment explaining why.
* v4.36 (wave 12): `omega` ignores list equalities like `h : os.entries = entries` — `rw` first; after `split at h` prefer `next a b hn =>` to `rename_i`; `simp only [h, ↓reduceIte]` replaces `if_pos/if_neg`.
* Unambiguity (2.5): `Spec.Unambiguous.resolveEntry limits bytes n`; `resolveEntry_eq_some_iff` (newest revision wins, index form), `resolveEntry_winner_unique`, `mergeChain_keys_nodup`/`mergeChain_mem_unique`, `resolveEntry_hybrid_table`/`_stream`, `checkSize` + `resolveEntry_lt_size`; `loadChain_update` (incremental update prepends one revision) holds under a `SameAt` hypothesis at old offsets — not yet discharged (task 2.12); update must not itself be hybrid. `/Size` is NOT enforced by `loadChain` for classic tables (only by xref streams) — `checkSize` composes in 2.12.
* Refactor (2.10): `ObjParse.parseHeader`, `parseIndirectBodyWith`/`parseStreamBodyWith` take `LengthResolver := Dict → Nat → Except Error Nat` (Nat = error offset); `Length.parseIndirectResolved` = header + body with `lengthOf`; `Lex.tokenAt_pos` (`next` unfolds to `tokenAt (skipWs …)`). Repair retries indirect-/Length streams once via a lazily built header table (`headerTable`, binary-search `tableLookup`), charged to the failed attempt (`scanAttempts_le` unchanged). Unproven cost: a successful retry may do up to 1 + t length-object parses (documented in Repair "Bounds"). Error order changed in `parseIndirectResolved` (EOL check before /Length resolution).
* qpdf lists free objects in its JSON as `{"value": null}` — the D-001 rule now also accepts a dangling target present on the qpdf side with value null (CI run 44410513896: timeout gone, 67,441/67,473 agree, all 32 disagreements were this case).
* v4.36 (2.5): `dite_eq_left_of_eq_true`, `dite_eq_right_of_eq_false`, `ite_eq_right_of_eq_false` take `eq_true h`/`eq_false h`; `unfold` of a match-defined function doesn't reduce on cons — add a `f_cons := rfl` lemma.
* Open (2.11): `Core.Open.openFile limits bytes : Except Error OpenDoc` (strict `loadChain`+`mergeChain`, else `Repair.repair`; both fail → strict error); `OpenDoc.getObject d limits n g` (binary search on `index`, free/absent/gen-mismatch → `.obj .null`); `openFile_strict`, `openFile_repaired`, `openFile_root`, `getObject_depth`, `getObject_work`; `Document` = header fields + `doc : OpenDoc` + `root`.
* Depth after 2.10: use `Core.Open.parseIndirectBodyWith_depth` (any `LengthResolver`; stream dict via `ObjParse.parseStreamBodyWith_ok`'s `s.dict = d`) — `Length.parseBodyResolved` no longer exists.
* Repair scans only classic `N G obj` headers: a repaired file whose `/Root` lives in an object stream fails validation. Hybrid merge puts the table first, so a table `free` entry hides an XRefStm `compressed` one (not seen in the corpus).
* Core unit checks: `#guard` (not `native_decide`) so no extra axiom appears.
* `.gitignore`'s `corpus/files/` does not match a symlink `corpus/files` — never symlink it in a worktree, it gets committed.
* Cycle errors (2.13): `Error.cycle (offset) (what)` + `Error.isCycle` (constructor test, never message text); produced by `Chain.step` (`/Prev`, `/XRefStm` revisit), `Stream.mkEntry` (object in its own objstm), `ObjStm.fetchCompressedTrace` (self/mutual container), `Length.resolveLengthAux`; `openFile` returns a strict cycle error without repair (rule P5; also for cycles found while validating /Root). Theorems `openFile_cycle`, `XrefTerm.loadChain_endless_cycle`, `openFile_endless` (needs `bytes.size ≤ maxRevisions`; longer `/Prev` tails hit `limitExceeded .revisions`, which still repairs).
* Adding an `Error` constructor breaks private `errS`/`summ` test helpers in ~8 files (ObjParse.lean included) — grep `| .unsupported w`.
* v4.36: a dependent `if` around a structure literal (`if … then ⟨0,0,.error a⟩ else ⟨…⟩`) breaks `cases h`/`split` with 'Dependent elimination failed' — move the `if` inside: `⟨0,0,.error (if … then a else b)⟩`.
* Xref views (2.8): `inspect --view xref|revisions|repair [--json]` in `Shell/Views/Xref.lean`; `xref` re-walks via `Chain.parseSection`/`hybridStm`/`Stream.parseXrefStream` and prints `trace mismatch` if it disagrees with `loadChain`; golden suite `xref-views` (`Tests/XrefViews.lean`, `Tests/golden/xref/`). Corpus: strict and repair agree on all 74 files (0 offset mismatches). No per-object trace view yet (CLI has no view arguments).
* `SAFEPDF_GOLDEN_UPDATE=1 lake exe tests` rewrites every golden suite — check `git status` afterwards. Goldens recorded in one worktree can go stale when another task of the same wave changes error rendering; regenerate at integration and review.
* Byte filters (3.3): `Core.Filter.Ascii` holds a shared byte-machine engine (`run` impl, `runSpec` spec, `run_spec`, `decodeWith_size_le`, `decodeWith_law`) used by `asciiHexDecode`, `ascii85Decode` and `RunLength.runLengthDecode`; each takes `(strict := true)` (lenient: end of input = EOD); bounds out ≤ ⌈in/2⌉, ≤ 4·in, ≤ 128·in; round trips with simple encoders. Engine would read better as `Filter/ByteMachine.lean` (refactor when 3.5 wires the pipeline).
* v4.36 (3.3): `∀ b : UInt8, P b` by `decide` is fast under `set_option maxRecDepth 100000 in` (bounded `∀ n < 256` over Nat overflows); `rw [lemma _ _ (by omega)]` fails to synthesize — use `?_` then `all_goals first | omega | (simp; omega) | simp`; `omega` fails on nested `/`/`%` — bound the value first, then `Nat.div_eq_of_lt_le`.
* `gh pr checks N` mixes in runs of the same commit on other branches (e.g. cancelled runs on the next phase branch after a cut) — check `gh run list --commit <sha>` and read the PR branch's own runs.
* Worktree subagents: Bash commands that `cd` into the main checkout or hold its paths in shell variables are refused; plain absolute paths for read-only access work.
* `/Size` + prefix stability (2.12): `Core.Open.checkSize` runs in `strictOpen` after `loadChain` (failure → repair); `strictOpen_ok`, `strictOpen_lt_size`, `Spec.Unambiguous.openFile_lt_size`. `Spec.PrefixStable`: `Extends b b'`, `*_ext` lemmas for lexer/object/indirect/xref table/xref stream parses (successful parses only), `section_stable`/`stream_stable` = `SameAt`; `loadChain_append`, `resolveEntry_append`, hybrid `loadChain_update_hybrid`/`resolveEntry_append_hybrid` — `SameAt` fully discharged.
* `lake env lean` on one file ignores the lakefile's `autoImplicit = false`: a misspelled type silently becomes an auto-bound variable — confirm with `lake build`.
* Prefix-stability proofs: error branches carry `errAt bytes pos` — rewrite with `errAt_ext` first; with `match _h : …` in a parser, `split` the goal and use the introduced equation; `rw [f]` fails on mutual-block functions → `f.eq_def`; `parseXrefStream`'s do-block becomes nested matches after `simp only [throw, throwThe, MonadExceptOf.throw, bind, Except.bind, pure, Except.pure]`.
* A subagent stopped by a transient API error (HTTP 522) keeps its uncommitted worktree: resume it with SendMessage to its agent id rather than respawning.
* LZW (3.4): `Core.Filter.LZW.lzwDecode limits input (early := true) (strict := true)`; `earlyOfDict` reads `/EarlyChange` (0/1, else malformed); table `Array Entry` (`pre`, `last`), `expand` by well-founded recursion with a runtime prefix<entry check; loop structural on `workBound = size*8/9 + 1` (`lzwDecode_fuel_enough`: fuel never decides the result); `lzwDecode_size_le`, `step_wf`, `expand_ok`. Missing EOD is accepted in both modes. Total-work O(input+output) is argued, not mechanised. Not yet called from any filter pipeline (3.5).
* v4.36 (3.4): `prefix` is a keyword (not a field name); `rw [getElem?_pos tab i hi]` on `Array` can hit max recursion — use `Array.getElem?_eq_getElem hi`; no `Nat.strong_induction_on` — use a self-calling theorem with `termination_by`; after `subst` of a structure literal run `dsimp only` before `omega`.
* `/Length` through object streams (2.14): decoding moved to `Core/Xref/ObjStmDecode.lean` (same namespace `Core.Xref.ObjStm`); `Length` imports it, `ObjStm` imports `Length`. `resolveLengthAux` rule L6 follows `compressed` entries (container `inUse off 0`, its own `/Length` resolved with `c :: n :: visiting`); fuel is now a shared work budget (well-founded on fuel) — may give `limitExceeded` on pathological files. `resolveLength_container_cycle` needs fuel `+1`.
* v4.36 (2.14): strong induction is `induction n using Nat.strongRecOn`; after `rw [f.eq_def]` a `let` shows up as `have` and blocks `split` — `dsimp only`; eta-reduced lemma args (`f`) vs goal lambdas (`fun v r => …`) look different to `omega` — state the `have` in lambda form.
* Worktree subagents cannot pass paths containing the main checkout (`…/gitprojects/safe-pdf/corpus`) to commands; tell them to copy corpus files into their scratchpad first.
* Filter pipeline (3.5): `Core.Filter.decodeStream limits d raw (strict := true) : Except Error Decoded` (`.data bytes` | `.image codec parms bytes` placeholder for DCT/JPX/JBIG2/CCITT, S7); `parseChain` (name/array, `/DecodeParms` dict only for one filter, array length must match, abbreviations accepted, indirect `/Filter` → `unsupported`: callers resolve first), `maxFilters = 8` (→ `unsupported` until 3.9); `/Crypt` Identity passes; theorems `decodeStream_size_le`, `decodeStream_nil`, `decodeStream_image`, `decodeStream_unknown`, `produced_le` (chain allocates ≤ n × limit).
* Deflate (3.8): `Core.Filter.Deflate.deflateStored`, `deflateFixed (lz77 := true)`, `zlibWrap`, `zlibStored`/`zlibFixed`; proved `inflate_deflateStored`, `zlibDecode_zlibStored` (any size ≤ limit); fixed-Huffman round trip tested only (suite `flate-roundtrip`, 217 inputs × 3 encoders, ~35 s compiled — the slowest suite). Use these encoders to build bomb inputs (3.6).
* Page tree (4.1): `Core.Doc.openDoc limits bytes : Except Error Doc`; `Doc.getPage (i : Fin doc.pages.size)` total; rules W1–W7/A1–A7 in the module doc: shared kid or `/Kids` cycle → `Error.cycle` (qpdf duplicates shared pages instead — watch in 4.4), dangling kid skipped (`skippedKids`), missing MediaBox → Letter + `mediaBoxDefaulted`, bad `/Rotate` → 0, CropBox clipped to MediaBox (disjoint → MediaBox), `/Count` advisory (`countMismatch`), attributes resolved one level.
* Crypto (4.3a): `Core.Crypt.Md5.md5`, `Sha2.sha256/384/512`, `Rc4.rc4 key data` (empty key = `0x00`), `Aes.expandKey` (16/24/32), `cbcDecrypt key iv data (pad)` (strict PKCS#7), `cbcEncryptNoPad`, `ecbDecrypt/ecbEncrypt`; all vector-tested (RFC 1321, FIPS 180-4, RFC 6229, FIPS 197, SP 800-38A); interpreted AES-256-CBC ≈ 0.2 MB/s.
* v4.36 (wave 16): `Dict` is an `abbrev` for `List` — write `Dict.get? d k`, not `d.get? k`; `List.asString` deprecated → `String.ofList`; no `Nat.iterate` → `Nat.repeat`; `ByteArray.foldl` has no core lemmas — unfold to `foldlM.loop` and induct on fuel (`Deflate.foldl_inv`); discharge a `dite` condition with `rw [dite_eq_left_of_eq_true (eq_true h)]`, rewrite under subtype proofs with `simp only [e]`; `Array.setIfInBounds` for in-place permutations without `!`; `cases h : a.f <;> simp [h]` instead of bare `cases`.
* Checking a new Tests file against an unbuilt new module: `LEAN_PATH` takes the first root containing `SafePdf/`, so build a private symlinked mirror of all oleans plus the new one — and never `cp` into another agent's mirror (it writes through the symlinks into `.lake/build`).
* CI flake: the `differential-objects` job can fail at `apt-get install qpdf` with HTTP 503 from the Ubuntu mirror (run 44522831417) — not a code failure; re-run the failed job and use the same commit's other run as evidence.
* Filter consolidation (3.9): `Limits.maxFilters := 8` + `LimitKind.filters` (exceeding → `limitExceeded .filters`); `/Crypt` not first → malformed; `Filter.decodeBytes limits d raw imageErr` (image codec → caller's error); xref streams reject `/Crypt` and image codecs (§7.6.2), object streams accept `/Crypt` first only; `decodeStream_unknown_name` now needs `1 ≤ limits.maxFilters`; new `decodeStream_too_many`, `parseChain_crypt_first`, `decodeBytes_size_le`, `Xref.Stream.decodeData_size_le`, `Xref.ObjStm.decodeData_size_le`. Corpus resolve output identical before/after.
* Streams view (3.7): `inspect --view streams [--json] FILE`; `SAFEPDF_STREAM=N` restricts to object N (`--json` adds `hex`, first 1 MiB), `SAFEPDF_STREAM_OUT=path` writes the full decoded bytes (use for 3.6b); resolves indirect `/Filter`/`/DecodeParms` one level; per-run budget `maxDecodedTotalBytes`. Corpus: 16,367 streams, 0 decode errors, 766 image placeholders, 1,454 MB decoded; slowest file 11.5 s (arxiv-2307.09288) — Flate speed is the bottleneck (Plan open question "Inflate performance in Lean").
* Golden suites treat EVERY file in `Tests/golden/<suite>/` as an input — keep generators outside (e.g. `Tests/golden/generate_streams.py`).
* v4.36 (3.7): `String.trim` gone → `s.trimAscii.toString`; no `ToString Prop` → `toString (decide (a > b))`; macOS `date +%N` doesn't work — time with Python `time.monotonic`.
* Flate work bound (3.8b): `Spec.FlateWork` — counted twins (`flateDecodeW`, `inflateAtW`, …) with `…W_fst` equal to the real decoder; `flateDecodeW_steps_le`: steps ≤ 536·input.size + 3·maxDecodedStreamBytes + 1923 for ANY outcome (potential 67/bit + 2/byte; dynamic block ≤ 1922 extra, ≥ 29 bits). Table building is a cost model (`buildCost n = 4n+64`), not twinned.
* Compiled `flateDecode` throughput (3.8b): zeros ≈ 84 MiB/s, stored ≈ 110 MiB/s, dynamic-Huffman text ≈ 6 MiB/s (bit-by-bit `decodeGo`) → task 3.11. A 256 MiB-limit bomb of text-like data costs ~40 s before rejection.
* v4.36 (3.8b): `split` on a `match` only generalizes the LHS — use `rcases <scrutinee> with …`; matches in two defs compile to different `match_i` auxiliaries, so `rw [heq]` between them fails — case-split the pieces; `set_option … in` goes before the docstring; `(a, b).fst` blocks `omega` — `dsimp only`; `readDynamic`'s do-block unfolds with `simp only [bind, Except.bind]`.
* Bomb suite (3.6a): `Tests/adversarial/bombs/` (`generate.py [--check]`, stdlib zlib — output depends on the linked zlib version, `--check` prints it on mismatch; 53 files, 4.5 MB) + suite `bombs` (`Tests/Bombs.lean`): CI runs the 1 MiB-limit mode (0.3 s); `SAFEPDF_BOMBS_DEFAULT=1` adds the 256 MiB-default run (~15 min because of LZW); `SAFEPDF_BOMBS_VERBOSE=1` per-row timings. All bombs give `limitExceeded` (chain-9 accepts `limitExceeded .filters` or `unsupported`). Default-limit times: Flate/RL/A85/chains 1–2 s, LZW 150–300 s (→ 3.12). `xref-bomb.pdf` opens via repair; `objstm-bomb-root.pdf` fails `openFile` with `limitExceeded`.
* RunLength's real max ratio is 64:1 per 2 bytes (not 128:1) and ASCII85 `z` is 4:1 — bombs of those kinds need a Flate wrapper to reach large ratios.
* The fuzzer skips `Tests/adversarial/bombs/` (bombs decode up to the limit by design and would always be slow inputs); they are covered by the `bombs` suite.
* Lean MCP server `lean-lsp` (lean-lsp-mcp 0.33.0 via `/opt/homebrew/bin/uvx`, `.mcp.json`): 22 tools (`lean_goal`, `lean_diagnostic_messages`, `lean_multi_attempt`, `lean_run_code`, `lean_hover_info`, `lean_local_search`, `lean_loogle`, …). It runs `lake serve` in the MAIN checkout: use read-only tools only while no `lake build` runs there, never `lean_build` (CLAUDE.md). Worktree subagents keep using `lake build`/`lake env lean`. Available only after a session restart + approval.
* A finished subagent's worktree can stay locked (`git worktree remove` refuses) — `git worktree unlock <path>` first.
* LZW speed (3.12): `step` now returns the output buffer in every outcome (`Action.done out | cont st | stop e out`), so `loop` never reads the old state after a step; bombs at 256 MiB: `lzw-kwkwk` 298 s → 2.7 s, `lzw-full-table` 150 s → 2.3 s (≈ 100 MB/s, linear); new theorem `step_error` (only `limitErr` escapes `step`). Default-limit bomb run: 1 m 18 s total, every call ≤ 3.3 s; `Tests/Bombs.lean` default bound tightened 60 s → 10 s.
* Lean 4 in-place updates: any later use of a field of the old state (even in another match arm reached only on EOD/error) makes the compiler `inc` that field before the call, so the `ByteArray` is shared and every append copies it (quadratic). Return the buffer from the step in every outcome; confirm with `set_option trace.compiler.ir.result true` (look for `inc` of the buffer before the call).
* Document budget (3.10): `Filter.Budget {remaining}`, `Budget.init limits` = `maxDecodedTotalBytes`; `decodeStreamB limits budget d raw : (Except Error Decoded) × Budget` (budget returned on failure too); each byte stage capped at `min maxDecodedStreamBytes remaining` and charged its output, a failing stage charged its cap, no refunds; `.decodedTotalBytes` when the budget binds; `decodeAllB_total`/`decodeAllB_init` (Σ outputs ≤ maxDecodedTotalBytes); Doc-level `Doc.decodeStreamOf`, `Doc.decodeContentsB` (page `/Contents`, parts unconcatenated) with `_total` theorems. Xref/object-stream decodes are NOT charged yet → 3.13.
* `String.fromUTF8!` trips the hygiene `!` rule — use `String.fromUTF8?` + `getD`; lambdas inside `s!` need explicit binder types; `split` fails when the scrutinee is behind a `let`/`have` in the goal — inline it.
* Inflate speed (3.11): 9-bit lookup table in `Huffman` (`table`, proof field `table_ok`, entries defined by the bit-by-bit loop `decodeGoB`/`tableEntry`), `decodeSym_eq : decodeSym … = decodeGo …` (slow decoder stays the spec); GMP-free `readBits` (`twoPow` table, `* 2` instead of `<<<`); Adler-32 as tail loop `adlerGo` (`adler32_eq_foldl`). Compiled MiB/s before → after: text 4.5 → 78, zeros 82 → 330, stored 111 → 418. `lake exe tests` 42 s → 16 s. Bench: `scripts/bench-flate.sh [--reps N] file.z…` (links with `leanc -O3` into `.lake/bench/`, no lakefile change).
* Compiled `Nat <<<` and `Nat ^` go through GMP even for small values (malloc/free per op) — use `*` by constants, a power table, or `>>>`; profile with macOS `sample`.
* `omega` can hit `maxRecDepth` with constants ~10⁴ across several hypotheses — `set_option maxRecDepth 200000 in`; `lake env lean` refuses files outside the package root unless `--root=<dir>`.
* zlib differential (3.6b): `python3 -I Tests/differential/run.py --comparator streams [--files 'corpus/files/X*.pdf'] [--per-file N]` (`--files` is a glob relative to cwd); plain `[FlateDecode]` streams without `/DecodeParms` only; raw bytes located by scanning `N G obj … stream` and taking the view's raw length; size check for every stream + byte compare for a sample (`--per-file`, default 20 — a full corpus run takes ~35 min, one inspector process per sampled stream). Corpus (pre-3.11 decoder): 12,748 plain-Flate streams, 1,476 byte-compared (111 MB), 0 disagreements, 0 size mismatches; 43 streams with trailing bytes after the zlib end decode identically. Offline mode: `streams.py --ours F --raw F`.
* Navigation (4.2): `Core.Doc.Nav` — never fails the document (rules N1–N8, `warnings` counts); generic name/number-tree walk `treeWalk`/`treeLookup` (visited set, fuel objectCount, `/Limits` pruning); `Dest n` (page `Option (Fin n)`, `Fit`), `Target` (dest | unresolved name | action kind, e.g. URI/GoToR kept, not followed); `outline doc limits` flat preorder with depths; `pageLinks doc limits i`; `pageLabel doc limits i` (roman/letters capped at 1..3999, else decimal). Theorems `treeWalk_visited_le`, `outline_items_le` (≤ objectCount), `*_nodup`. Lookup worst case objectCount × lookups (bounded, quadratic).
* Name-tree `/Limits` compare raw bytes: `(zeta) > (z)`, so `/Limits [(n) (z)]` excludes `zeta`.
* Page-tree proofs (4.1b): `Spec.PageTree` (`SafePdf/Spec/Doc.lean`) — tree view `KidsOf`/`Kid`/`KidPath`/`Descends`/`LeafNode`, `nearest f path`; `openDoc_path_nodup`, `walk_kid_not_ancestor`, `openDoc_cycle_error` (fails, error kind not pinned), `openDoc_page_inherit` (every page's boxes/rotate/resources/contents = nearest ancestor's along SOME root path), `walk_fuel_irrelevant`, `popLive_exhausted`, `getObject_ne_null_key` (non-null ⇒ an xref index key, via `bsearch_key`). Gaps → 4.1c.
* v4.36 (4.1b): `h ▸ hv` fails when `h : v = (a, b).fst` — `rw [h] at hv`; `rw [Core.Open.bsearch.eq_def]` for the `termination_by` binary search.
* Structural decode budget (3.13): `Open.Session {doc, budget, cache}` (`Session.open`, `Session.getObject`), `openFileB`, `getObjectB`, `Chain.loadChainB`, `ObjStm.fetchCompressedB` with an object-stream cache (`CacheExact`; each container decoded once per session); `capped_of_le`: while remaining ≥ maxDecodedStreamBytes every stage runs under exactly `limits`, giving real equalities `loadChainB_eq`, `openFileB_eq`, `getObjectB_eq` — but their budget preconditions exceed 1 GiB with default limits, so they hold only for large budgets. `Core.Filter.Structural.decodeBytesB`. Uncharged: `/Length` L6 object-stream decodes; `openDoc`/`Parse` still unbudgeted → 4.5.
* v4.36 (3.13): a do-block function equals its split pieces by `cases` on each intermediate + `simp only [bind, Except.bind, pure, Except.pure, throw, throwThe, MonadExceptOf.throw]`; two matches on the same scrutinee with different matchers aren't closed by `rfl` — `cases x <;> rfl`; `split` leaves bound variables inaccessible — `rcases h : e with …`; `by_cases hg : g = 0` + `simp [hg]` doesn't substitute later — `subst hg`.
* Graphics types (5.1): `Core.Content` — `Fixed` = raw·10⁻⁶ with proved bound |raw| ≤ 10¹⁸ (saturating `clamp`), round half up (`roundDiv`, Int ediv), matrix entries computed at 10¹² and rounded once — `Matrix.mul` NOT associative under rounding: compose left to right as ISO writes it (`cm`: CTM' = M × CTM). Paths in page space (CTM applied at construction; strokes carry the CTM; no `rect` segment — `re` expands to moves/lines). `Clip := List ClipPath` newest first; `Tm`/`Tlm` in `TextMatrices` outside `GState` (§9.4.1: q/Q don't save them); `Q` on empty stack ignored (`restore_empty`), `q` at `Limits.maxGStateDepth` (256) → `limitExceeded .gstateDepth`. `DisplayItem` = fillPath/strokePath/clip/glyphRun/image/shading/unsupported; `Paint.items` (fill+stroke = 2 items). Glyph advance needs widths → 5.2 takes an advance function (zero stub for M5).
* v4.36 (5.1): `dif_neg` deprecated — `split` then `omega`; `omega` can't see through projections of anonymous constructors — `dsimp only at h`; unfold constant defs (e.g. `fixedScale`) before `omega`; to check an importer against a scratch olean run `lean` directly with `LEAN_PATH=$S/lib:$(lake env printenv LEAN_PATH)`; structures holding `ByteArray`/`Dict` can't derive `BEq`.
* Page-tree gaps closed (4.1c): `Spec.PageTree.openDoc_page_path_unique` (live nodes have one root path, so `nearest` in `openDoc_page_inherit` is well defined; dangling `null` kids may be shared), `openDoc_cycle_error_kind` / `openDoc_shared_error` (with `WellFormedTree` — every node on a duplicate-free path is null or a well-formed dict within depth — a `/Kids` cycle or shared live kid gives `Error.cycle` specifically); key lemma `loop_forest`.
* v4.36 (4.1c): after `rcases … with rfl | hx <;> …` use fresh names (`hx'`); `cases hb'` on `obj (dict nd) = obj (dict nd')` removes `nd'` — write `⟨_, hg, ho⟩`; `Except.ok.inj`.
* Pages/outline views + qpdf pages comparator (4.4): `inspect --view pages|outline [--json]` (`Shell/Views/Pages.lean`, golden suite `pages-view`); `Tests/differential/pages.py` (comparator `pages`, run in CI step "Pages comparison on the corpus" with `--min-compared 20`): qpdf side `--json=2 --json-key=pages --json-key=qpdf`, boxes computed from qpdf's raw objects by walking `/Parent` with our rules A1–A6; statuses agree/known/disagree (known = shared-kid rejection, D-002). qpdf JSON assumptions UNVERIFIED until the first CI run. Corpus locally: 2,023 pages, 0 /Count mismatches, 46 outlines (1,169 items), 17,341 links, slowest 1.4 s. Use `Nav.pageLabels` (one walk) not `pageLabel` per page.
* Security handler (4.3b): `Core.Crypt` — V1 R2, V2 R3, V4 R4 (`/CF`: V2/AESV2/None), V5 R5/R6 (AESV3), empty user password only (non-empty → `unsupported`); `/P` advisory (S8), `/Perms` → `State.permsOk`. Decrypted in `getObject`/`getObjectB`/`Session`: in-use objects' strings and stream data; object-stream containers decrypted once (`ObjStm.Pre`, rule O7), members not again. Not decrypted: the `/Encrypt` object, xref streams, `/Metadata` when `/EncryptMetadata false`, `/Crypt /Identity` streams. Fixtures `Tests/adversarial/encrypted/` (independent pure-Python RC4/AES, `--check`), suite `crypt`. Corpus has NO encrypted files — M4's "encrypted publisher PDFs open" needs real encrypted files (only our generated ones today).
* Repair builds its own trailer for xref-stream-only files without `/Encrypt`: an encrypted file that falls back to repair opens undecrypted (→ 4.6).
* v4.36 (4.3b): `split` on a `let`-bound `if` needs `simp only at h` first; `obtain ⟨-, …, hw, -⟩` fails when `hw` depends on a cleared component — use `_`.
* Content interpreter core (5.2a): `Core.Content.Ops.tokenize limits bytes` (`Op {operands, op, offset, data}`; arrays/dicts via `ObjParse.parseObjTok`; never fails — lexer errors skip to whitespace, unterminated strings end the stream, malformed arrays resume at the error token; inline images `BI…ID…EI` via `/L`/`/Length` or a byte scan for whitespace-`EI`-delimiter; `maxOpsPerPage` cap → `truncated`); `interp cfg ops` = `Array.foldl exec` (`Config {limits, advance := 0}`), `interpBytes`; `q` at the depth limit skipped + warning, `Q` on empty ignored + warning, empty-path paint emits nothing, `W` clip applies after the painting op; `Do`/`sh`/`BI` are placeholders; unknown ops → `unsupported` items (warning outside `BX`/`EX`). Theorems `interp_items_le` (≤ 2 × ops), `interpBytes_items_le`, `runOps_stack_bounded`, `tokenize_ops_le(_max)`. Linear scaling measured. Worst case: nested malformed arrays O(maxObjectDepth · n).
* Scratch-checking a file that imports an unbuilt new module: Lean resolves the package at the first `SafePdf/` on `LEAN_PATH`, so the scratch lib must hold the whole `SafePdf/` tree (symlink the built `.olean`s, add the new one) — never write under `.lake/`. `List UInt8` literal patterns (`| [99, 109] =>`) dispatch operators cheaply.
* Encrypted corpus subset (4.7): 7 public-domain US government PDFs (NTSB AAR0501 RC4-40, AAR0701/RAR0801 RC4-128, AAR1201 AES-128, AIR2201/MIR2301 AES-256 R6 — with object streams; USCIS g-1145 AES-128 linearized). NTSB years: RC4-40 2005, RC4-128 2006–08, AES-128 2012–20, AES-256 2021+. `fetch.py --add` reads encrypted `/Producer` bytes — manifest corrected by hand (`category.encryption`); `--reindex` would undo it. 6/6 NTSB files open strict and decrypt (pages = /Count, 0 stream errors, outlines readable).
* Signature dictionaries' `/Contents` strings are NOT encrypted (§7.6.2): decrypting them fails PKCS#7 padding and broke the strict open of USCIS g-1145 (catalog `/Perms /UR3` with `/ByteRange`) → sent to 4.6.
* Encryption follow-ups (4.6): `Error.encryption (what)` + `Error.isEncryption` + `Error.isFinal` (cycle ∨ encryption) — `openFile`/`openFileB` return final strict errors without repair; rule E3: strict non-final + repair encryption error → the repair error; `Crypt.setupError` converts by constructor (prefix tagging removed). Repair rule R4': copy `/Encrypt` + `/ID` from the newest trailer dict (else newest `/Type /XRef` dict); `/Encrypt` lost → `Error.encryption`. `ObjStm.Pre` moved to `ObjStmDecode.lean`; `pre` threaded through `/Length` resolution (`parseIndirectResolved … (pre := Pre.id)`). Named `/Crypt` filters: `decryptBody` marks the applied stage `/Identity` (rule C7'). Signature dicts (`/Type /Sig|/DocTimeStamp` or `/ByteRange`): `/Contents` not decrypted (C7''); other string-decrypt failures still fail the object. Corpus: 81/81 open strict, 7 encrypted, 0 regressions.
* macOS `sed` has no `\b`.
* The inspector `objects` view is a linear scan independent of the xref; for encrypted files it must decrypt with `doc.doc.decrypt num gen` (`decryptFound`), or the qpdf comparator sees ciphertext.
* Ops/SVG views + coverage (5.4): `inspect --view ops [--json]` (per page: decode contents under one budget, tokenize, run `Interp.exec` per op; covered = not reported unknown; warned = skipped/repaired with a warning), `--view svg` (`SAFEPDF_PAGE=N` to stdout, `SAFEPDF_SVG_DIR=dir` for all pages; CropBox frame, y flip + Rotate, exact `Fixed` numbers, strokes in user space via `transform=matrix(CTM)`, deduped `<clipPath>`, text/image/shading placeholders); golden suite `svg-view`; `python3 -I scripts/corpus/opcoverage.py --corpus DIR` → `docs/opcoverage.{md,json}`. Corpus (9bacf94): 81 files, 2,450 pages, 3,414,920 ops, covered 100.000%, strict (placeholders gs/Do/BI/sh/d0/d1 excluded) 99.672%, 0 failures, max 213 ms/page. Page content only — forms/Type 3/patterns/annotation appearances not counted until 5.2b. Zero-advance stub stacks consecutive `Tj`s (phase 6).
* SVG `clip-path` on an element with its own `transform` is read in the transformed space — put clips on a wrapping `<g>`.
* Interpreter spec (5.3): `Spec.Content.Step cfg : Machine → Op → Machine → List DisplayItem → Prop` (`defined` via `Effect` save/restore/paint/clip/simple with 43 per-operator `SimpleStep` constructors; `skip` for known-but-invalid ops; `unknown` → `unsupported`), all 71 known operators specified, no catch-all; `step_iff_exec`, `step_det`, `step_total`, `steps_iff_runOps`, `interp_steps`. Warning/unsupported counts in `Result` not specified. Proofs name the matcher `Interp.simple.match_32 (was match_33 before 6.8d)` — editing `Interp.simple` (5.2b) may require updating that name and `simpleKws`.
* v4.36 (5.3): a `match` with ~57 `List UInt8` literal patterns times out generating its equation lemmas/splitter (200000 heartbeats; `set_option maxHeartbeats` doesn't reach it) — reduce concrete cases with `conv at h => lhs; whnf`, handle fallthrough by `delta`-ing the matcher and walking its `dite` tree; `decide` needs `dsimp only` first on structure-literal membership goals; rewrite a function occurring twice via `generalize hs : f … = r` + `conv at hs => lhs; whnf` + `subst`.
* Orchestrator: `git add -A` while a shared-checkout subagent is still writing commits its partial file — stage only the integrated paths when a `new` subagent is still running.
* Session-wide accounting (4.5): budgeted `/Length` resolver (`resolveLengthAuxB`, `parseIndirectResolvedB`; `decodeObjStmB` moved to Length.lean) charges rule-L6 decodes (not cached: caching could turn a cycle into success); page-tree walk twins `walkB`/`ofOpenB`, `Doc.ofSession`, `Doc.openDocS` (one-directional `openDocS_eq`: success with ≥ 1 allowance left ⇒ equals `openDoc`); `Doc.decodeStreamS`/`decodeContentsS`; `Document.session` (parseWith uses `Session.open`), views Resolve/Pages/Streams share it. New equality pattern: "≥ one allowance left at the END ⇒ budgeted = unbudgeted". Still unbudgeted: `Core.Doc.Nav` walks (bounded by `getObject_work`), `Doc.decodeStreamOf`/`decodeContentsB` (kept for 5.4's SVG/ops views). Bomb `objstm-two.pdf` (two 768 KiB object streams) exercises the shared budget + cache.
* v4.36 (4.5): `rcases h : e` only rewrites the goal — revert dependent hypotheses first; `rw` can't match another definition's `match` (different matchers) — rewrite the discriminant equality; `simp only` turns `x = x` into `True` — close leaves with `first | rfl | trivial`; `generalize` a `let`-bound term only after `dsimp only`.
* Encodings/widths (6.4): `Core.Font` — `GlyphName := String` (Latin-1 bytes); `standardEncoding`, `winAnsiEncoding` (= cp1252 exactly), `macRomanEncoding`, `pdfDocEncoding` (256 each), `applyDifferences`, `simpleEncoding resolve fontDict builtin?`, `glyphToUnicode` (AGL rules, 248-name subset + uniXXXX/uXXXX); `Std14.width?`, `Std14.ofBaseFont` (subset prefix and Arial/TimesNewRoman/CourierNew substitution); `Widths`: `FontMetrics.ofDict` (simple: /Widths → /MissingWidth → std14 → 0; Type0 Identity-H/V: `/W` dense 65536 table, `/DW` 1000; Type3 and other CMaps → none), widths /1000 exact as `Fixed`. Symbol/ZapfDingbats missing → 6.4b. Every dict reader takes `resolve : PdfObject → PdfObject`.
* Type 1 fonts (6.3): `Core.Font.Type1.parse limits bytes (len1 len2)` → `Font1` (name, `fontMatrix`, bbox, encoding (256), glyphs with `hsbw`/`sbw`/`seac` metrics, `rejected` list, `advances` (256)); robust split (PFB, eexec search; `/Length1/2` only if consistent), hex eexec, `lenIV`; charstring interpreter for metrics only (path ops clear the stack; outlines in phase 7), bounded stack 24/subr depth 10/fuel 20000; `Font1.advance code : Option Fixed` (text space). Corpus smoke: 109 embedded Type 1 fonts, 7,652 glyphs, 0 rejected. Fixtures `Tests/fonts/gen_type1.py [OUTDIR]` (deterministic; tiny.t1/.pfb/.pfa). Some corpus PDFs give `/Length1` as an indirect reference.
* v4.36 (6.3/6.4): `rfl`/`simp` on the size of a 256-element `#[…]` literal hits recursion limits — `set_option maxRecDepth 4000 in` before the docstring + `rw [def, Array.size_map]; rfl`; Lean strings have no `\u{…}` escape — `String.ofList [Char.ofNat n]`; docstrings aren't allowed on `#guard`; `split at h` on a long `if` chain behind `have c := s[i]` exhausts simp steps — factor branches out; `ByteArray.emptyWithCapacity` sizes are awkward — use `ByteArray.empty`; no `swap` without Batteries.
* Content runner (5.2b): `Core.Content.Run` wraps `exec` (Interp and Spec/Content unchanged): `stepR` intercepts `Do`, `gs`, `CS/cs` on resource names, `SC/SCN/sc/scn` in resolved spaces; document access via `Source σ` (fetch + decode threading caller state); forms: `/Matrix`, `/BBox` clip, own or parent `/Resources`, machine restored after; images by reference (data not decoded); ExtGState LW/LC/LJ/ML/D/RI/CA/ca applied (`/Font` ignored); ICCBased/CalGray/CalRGB → device, Indexed/Separation(type-2 tint)/DeviceN/Lab → `.other` (placeholder colours for other tint functions); inline-image abbreviations expanded. Bounds `runPage_ops_le` (≤ maxOpsPerPage total), `runPage_items_le`; recursion fuel `maxXObjectDepth` + nesting-path visited set (`doStep_form_fresh`); per-page form-expansion cap `Run.defaultMaxForms` = 100,000 (fan-out bomb 31 s → 0.7 s). Every `Do` re-fetches/decodes/tokenizes the form (no cache yet).
* `lake env lean` checks a file against possibly stale `.olean`s of edited imports — rebuild the imported module first; after `open Run`, write `Run.runOps` (clashes with `Interp.runOps`); an `Env` with default fields can't be built with `⟨…⟩`.
* TrueType (6.1): `Core.Font.TrueType.parse limits bytes` → `TTFont` (`head`/`maxp` required; `hhea`+`hmtx`, `cmap` 0/4/6/12, `post` 1.0/2.0, `loca`+`glyf` optional; bad glyphs → `rejected`, composites resolved in 17 rounds, cycles rejected); `advanceOfGlyph`, `glyphOfCode code symbolic enc` (§9.6.6.4), `glyphOfUnicode`, `glyphName?`, `advanceText`, `outline gid` (quadratic contours, font units, y up). OTTO/ttcf → unsupported; checksums/hinting not done. Corpus: 8 `/FontFile2` fonts parse, 0 rejected; some have no `cmap` (CID-keyed → need `/CIDToGIDMap`). Fixtures `Tests/fonts/gen_truetype.py --check`.
* Text extraction (6.5): `Core.Font.CMap.parse` (lenient ToUnicode: codespace 1–4 bytes, bfchar/bfrange incl. array form and carry, `usecmap` recorded not followed; caps `maxTokens` 2²⁰, `maxEntries` 2¹⁷); `Core.Text.extractDocPage limits doc page budget` (fonts via `loadFonts`, real advances fed to `Config.advance`, mapping ToUnicode → encoding+AGL → ASCII 32..126 for simple fonts → UTF-16 for UCS2 CMaps → U+FFFD; ligatures FB00–FB06 decomposed); reading order heuristic in `Options` (`wordGap` 0.12 em, `lineTol` 50%, `paraGap` 2 em; lines keep content order, glyphs sorted along the baseline, per-glyph rotation frames). C6/C3: `extract_from_items`, `runGlyphs_codes`, `extractPage_glyphs`/`_items` (by `rfl`). Corpus page-1 smoke: 1706.03762, 1512.03385, 1409.0473 clean; 0704.0001 (Type 3 bitmap) mostly clean, accents → U+FFFD.
* v4.36 (6.5/6.1): `String.take` returns `String.Slice` (no `Repr`) — `String.ofList (s.toList.take n)`; `s.any fun c => …` needs `(c : Char)`; no `List.flatten_flatMap`; `match h : rest with` for well-founded nested patterns; nested do-pattern tuples `(a, b, p)` ≠ `((a, b), p)`; a module that isn't built can't be imported from a scratch file.
* Text view + pdftotext differential (6.6): `inspect --view text [--json]` (`SAFEPDF_PAGE=N`, `SAFEPDF_TEXT_GLYPHS=1` glyph dump; `SAFEPDF_SVG_TEXT=1` overlays glyph boxes in the svg view), golden suite `text-view`; `Tests/differential/text.py` (comparator `text`): reference `pdftotext -enc UTF-8` split on form feeds, normalization NFKC + drop soft hyphens/zero-width/controls + fold dashes/quotes (`--no-fold`) + collapse whitespace, similarity = 1 − Levenshtein/max (bit-parallel exact; word-level fallback above 100k code points), character-weighted corpus similarity, `--min-similarity` (not set in CI). Corpus locally: 2,450 pages, 7.0 M chars, 75,431 U+FFFD (most in NTSB AAR0701/AAR0501), slowest file 9.4 s. `inspect` exits 0 even when the page tree is rejected (error only in the JSON summary).
* Text comparators: `run.py --comparator text` (pdftotext) and `--comparator text-mupdf` (`mutool draw -q -F txt -o - FILE`; MuPDF also ends every page with a form feed); one text.py instance per reference so each keeps its own worst-pages list; JSON keys `text` and `text-mupdf`. Local tools: `pdftotext` 26.11.0 and `mutool` 1.28.6 (Homebrew). Sample: 1706.03762 → 0.9350 vs pdftotext, 0.9703 vs MuPDF (7/15 pages exact).
* Linux sandbox (2026-11-20): `corpus/files/` is NOT present (only `manifest.json`) — run `python3 -I scripts/corpus/fetch.py` once before corpus measurements; `mutool`/`pdftotext` not installed (apt `mupdf-tools`, `poppler-utils`); the Lean MCP tools are not configured (`.mcp.json` names a macOS path), so subagents fall back to `lake env lean`/`lake build`.
* Core v4.36 has `Rat` in `Init` with field lemmas (`Rat.mul_assoc`, `Rat.mul_inv_cancel`, …); `grind` closes field identities with `x⁻¹` given `det ≠ 0` but not nonlinear order facts (use `Rat.mul_nonneg`, `Rat.mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left`); `decide` does not reduce `Rat` division (use `grind`); `#guard` on `Rat` works; no `Rat.toFloat` (`Float.ofInt r.num / Float.ofNat r.den`) (7.1).
* Raster geometry (7.1): `Core.Raster.Geom` — `Pt`, `Aff` (row-vector convention) over `Rat`, `Aff.inv` with `inv_apply_apply`; reuses `Doc.Rotation` (+ `rotAdd`); `toScreen`/`toPage` (z, rot, box) with C2 `toPage_toScreen`/`toScreen_toPage` and view variants with user rotation; `Fixed.toRat`, `Aff.ofMatrix`, one-step `Content.Matrix.apply` error ≤ ½·10⁻⁶ (`Matrix.apply_x_err`) — no bound for chains of rounded products. Test suite `raster-geom` compares a test-only Float copy.
* A new test file importing a new, unbuilt module can be checked by concatenating both into a scratch file with `#eval run` (7.1).
* CFF (6.2): `Core.Font.CFF.parse limits bytes` → `CFFFont` (name- and CID-keyed; `advance code`, `advanceOfGid/CID`, `gidOfCode`, `gidOfCID`, `glyphName?`, `glyphNameOfCode`, `fontMatrix`); Type 2 interpreter for widths only (no outlines; `put get ifelse random sqrt index roll` reject the glyph); CFF2/OpenType-wrapped → unsupported; caps local (`fontFuel` 2²⁵ font-wide is the real work bound) until 6.2b. NOT yet used by `Core.Text` (6.2b). Fixtures `Tests/fonts/gen_cff.py` → `tiny.cff`, `tiny-cid.cff`. A subroutine bomb takes 33 s interpreted (`#eval`).
* Proof patterns (6.2): a runtime `offs.all (fun o => decide …)` check yields per-index proofs via `Array.all_eq_true` (cheap valid-by-construction subtypes); returning `{r : Nat // r ≤ fuel}` with a small `up` lift makes step bounds one-liners; in `do`, `let (a, b) := f x` loses the link to `f x` — use `let ev := f x` and `ev.1`.
* Font identity (6.7a): `GState.FontId` + defaulted `FontRef.id`; Run records the font object (indirect ref, or the owning page/form for inline font dicts) after `Tf` and for ExtGState `/Font`; Text keys its font table by `FontId` and collects fonts through forms recursively (≤ 64 resource dicts, 64 XObjects per dict), falling back to the page font of the same name. Suite `font-identity`. `Svg.lean` re-exports `Text.docSource`/`joinParts`. Construct `FontRef` with `{ name := n, size := s }` (anonymous `⟨n, s⟩` no longer fills the defaulted field).
* A `/-- -/` docstring before `export` is a parse error; after `rcases` on a tuple-valued call, `dsimp only at h ⊢` before `split` (6.7a).
* Symbol/ZapfDingbats (6.4b): `Encoding.symbolEncoding`/`zapfDingbatsEncoding` are the builtin fallback in `simpleEncoding` for those base fonts (after `stripSubsetTag`, now in Encoding); `glyphToUnicodeIn dingbats g` maps `aNN` names only for ZapfDingbats (other fonts reuse `a12`-style names); `Text.simpleUnicode` takes the `dingbats` flag. Tables written from memory — verify (6.4c). Type1 uses `Encoding.standardEncoding` (dedup checked, 149 entries).
* Limits (6.4b): `maxFontGlyphs 4096` (→ `limitExceeded .fontGlyphs`), `maxFontSubrs 32768` (→ `.fontSubrs`), `glyphFuel 20000` (glyph rejected, no error), `maxFormsPerPage 100000` (forms skipped + warning, no error); `Run.Env.maxForms` defaults to `cfg.limits.maxFormsPerPage` (a structure default may refer to an earlier field).
* CFF in text (6.2b): `/FontFile3 /Type1C` loads like an embedded Type 1 program (only when no Type 1 program): CFF encoding/charset is the built-in encoding (`/Differences` over it, AGL names), widths `/Widths` → CFF advance (256-entry table once per font); CIDFontType0C: `/W` → `/DW` → CFF advance of the CID → 1000 (program parsed only without `/DW`); `/OpenType` FontFile3 ignored. Limits `maxCFFGlyphs` 65535 (`.fontGlyphs`), `fontFuel` 2²⁵ (glyphs rejected, no error), `maxFontDicts` 256 (`LimitKind.fontDicts`), CFF Subrs share `maxFontSubrs` 32768. Suite `cff-text`. Test PDFs embed binary font programs as `/ASCIIHexDecode` streams (`String.toUTF8` corrupts bytes).
* Symbol.afm 001.008 encodes 189 codes: `apple` has no code (6.4b had it at 0xF0; fixed by 6.4c, `Std14.symbolAppleWidth` 790 for `/Differences`). The 6.4b tables were verified against PDFBox 3.0.3 AFMs + agl-aglfn 4036a9c (scripts in the wave-27 scratch dir, not committed); Euro widths come from the redistributed AFMs (Adobe's 1997 originals have no Euro). PDFBox AFMs have blank lines between lines.
* JSON lines (6.9): `Views/Common.jsonString` escapes U+2028/U+2029/U+0085/DEL as `\uXXXX`; differential readers (`text.py`, `objects.py`, `pages.py`) split our output on `"\n"` only — Python `str.splitlines()` also splits on 0x1C–0x1E, 0x85, U+2028/2029. Golden `Tests/golden/text/lineseps.pdf` (generator `Tests/golden/generate_text.py`).
* Fill core (7.2a): `Core.Raster.Fill` — `ofContentPath m p` (page path → integer device coords in 1/256 px via exact `Geom.Aff`, clamp ±2³⁰), `flatten` (n chords, smallest with 3M ≤ 4·tol·n², cap `curveSegCap` 512; per-coordinate bound `flatten_err_tol`, √2 for Euclidean; impl = model `bezNum_eq` + ½ unit rounding), `fillEdges`/`fillEdgesCounted` (active-edge scanline, `Config.vSamples` default 4 sample rows/px, exact horizontal coverage, nonzero/even-odd) → `Mask {x0,y0,w,h,data}`; `polygonEdges` (for 7.2b), `Mask.get/total` (7.2c), `DPath`/`DSeg` (7.3; quadratics via degree elevation), `fillPath m path rule clip`. Work `fillEdgesCounted_steps_le` ≤ h·(S·E + w + 1) (crossing sort not counted). Default tolerance 26/256 ≈ 0.1 px (0.2 px thinned a radius-5 disc by 4.5%). Interpreted: full 600×800 fill 212 ms; 1000 glyph-size circles 257 ms. `curveSegCap`/`coordLimit` local constants (→ `Limits` later). `SAFEPDF_PGM_DIR=dir` dumps PGMs in suite `raster-fill`.
* v4.36 Rat/Int (7.2a): no `Rat.div_le_iff` (use `Rat.not_lt.mp` + `Rat.lt_div_iff`); `Rat.le_total (a := 0) (b := x)`; `Array.size_mergeSort` exists (not for `qsort`/`insertionSort`); Int division bounds via `Int.mul_ediv_add_emod` + generalize + `omega`; `dsimp only` before `omega` when `let`s are in the goal.
* Network (2026-11-20): the sandbox firewall needs host approval for `arxiv.org`, `www.ntsb.gov`, `www.uscis.gov` (HTTP 403 "Approval required") — `fetch.py` fails every entry; `raw.githubusercontent.com` works. `mutool` 1.25.1 and `qpdf` 12.2.0 installed via apt in the sandbox.
* Worktree agents: a Bash command mentioning `github` together with `cd`, or chaining `git diff` into a loop, is refused by the worktree guard — use absolute paths and plain git commands (wave 27). Worktrees may start at `main` (2a7d341): the HEAD check in the brief caught it.
* Unicode mapping (6.7b): full Adobe Glyph List (4281 names) + lcdf-typetools `texglyphlist.txt` (271 TeX names) in `Core/Font/GlyphList.lean` (generated by `Tests/fonts/gen_agl.py`, sources pinned by SHA-256; one string literal parsed once into a sorted array, `termination_by hi - lo` binary search). `simpleUnicode dingbats type3 tu enc`: ASCII-at-code fallback only for Type 3 fonts (MuPDF rule; poppler guesses everywhere), Type 3 decimal `aNN` names (Ghostscript/dvips) for 32..0xFFFF; Type 1 program also read when a partial ToUnicode (pdfTeX writes one) leaves a non-zero-width code unmapped (`toUnicodeCovers`). Accents map to spacing accents (´ ¨ ˆ ¸), not composed (MuPDF prints `caf´e`). Suite `text-unicode`. All 75 Computer Modern fonts now match MuPDF code-for-code. pdfTeX: `\pdfgentounicode=0` produces a PDF without ToUnicode; texlive-latex-base + texlive-fonts-recommended installed in the sandbox.
* Layout (6.7c): CIDFontType2 widths `/W` → `/DW` → TrueType advance of the CID's glyph via `/CIDToGIDMap` (`/Identity` or 2-byte stream, `CIDToGID`, `loadTT`, `loadCIDToGID`) → 1000; `cidWidth` takes a `prog : Nat → Option Fixed` fallback; simple TrueType: codes not covered by `/Widths` → `TTFont.advanceText` (§9.6.6.4, symbolic bit from `/Flags`, `ttWidthTable`). Line grouping `scriptJoin` keeps raised/lowered smaller glyphs (≤ 0.6 × line size, within −0.5..+1 em of the line end) on their line (`Options.scriptRaise/scriptBack/scriptAhead`); lines sorted by x (MuPDF breaks when a superscript steps back: `Sum xi\n2`; we print `Sum xi2`). Suite `text-layout`. MuPDF keeps a raised small glyph on its line, poppler splits it beyond ~½ em.
* Stroking (7.2b): `Core.Raster.Stroke.strokeEdges view path style` / `strokePath … clip` — centre line flattened in device space; pen = two device offsets per chord (along chord + along user normal, user length w/2) so non-uniform CTMs give ellipses; caps/joins/miter limit in user space, reflecting CTMs via sign of det; dashes (odd arrays doubled, phase mod period, restart per subpath, zero-length = dot, all-zero/negative = solid); all pieces convex and equally oriented so nonzero fill = union; zero width or degenerate CTM → 1 px hairline pen; `sqrtQ` with relative error < 2⁻²⁰ (`sqrtQ_rel`); `strokeEdges_size_le`. Local caps `maxDashSteps` 100000 (beyond → solid), `arcSegCap` 64, `smoothMiterLimit` 4 (task 7.2d). Suite `raster-stroke`. Fractional widths under 7.2a's 4 vertical samples are coarse vertically (a 2.83 px horizontal line covers 3 px).
* Clipping/render (7.2c): `Core.Raster.Clip` — clip masks per `ClipPath` with its rule, intersection = coverage product `round(a·b/255)` (MuPDF), `ClipCache` 16 entries with tail reuse for nested clips; straight source-over into opaque white RGBA8 with `ca`/`CA`; Gray/RGB/naive CMYK, pattern → grey 128 placeholder; `renderPaths page zoom rot items (strokeMask := defaultStroke)` — `defaultStroke` is wired to `Stroke.strokePath` at integration (wave 28); `maxPageSide` 16384 (larger pages cropped). Text/images/shadings not painted yet (7.3). Theorems `covAt_le_mask/clip/255`, `renderPaths_size`, `renderItemsCounted_steps_le`. Suite `raster-clip` (`SAFEPDF_PPM_DIR`). Interpreted: 1000 rects under one clip ≈ 450 ms.
* Defining `IRect.foo`/`Mask.foo` in another namespace breaks dot notation — use `def _root_.SafePdf.Core.Raster.Fill.IRect.foo`; `UInt8.ofNatClamp` (+ `UInt8.toNat_ofNatClamp_of_lt`) for saturating bytes (`ofNatTruncate` deprecated); `Rat.ceil`/`Rat.floor`, `Nat.sqrt` (`Nat.sqrt_le`, `Nat.lt_succ_sqrt`) exist in core v4.36; `Rat.num_pos` doesn't (`Rat.num_nonneg` + `Rat.num_eq_zero`); concatenating files with `grep -v "^import"` also drops docstring lines starting with "import" (7.2b/7.2c).
* `adversarial` timing bound (`xobject-fanout.pdf` 2000 ms) once took 2208 ms on a loaded machine (wave 28, during parallel builds) — flaky under load; passes otherwise.
* MuPDF 1.25.1 sources are reachable at raw.githubusercontent.com (tag `1.25.1`, e.g. `source/fitz/stext-device.c`): read them to match MuPDF behaviour exactly instead of guessing (6.7d).
* Text order/spacing (6.7d): a glyph moving back but overlapping the previous one (within `Options.attachBack` 800‰ of its advance end) takes its sort key (accents before letters, MuPDF content order); spaces follow MuPDF `may_add_space` (`spaceMayFollow`: no space after a char ≥ U+0700 outside U+2000–U+20CF, e.g. `−`, `≤`, `∞`, `→`), `wordGap` 150‰.
