Known differences: right-to-left drawn words (we print `Hello World`, MuPDF splits), underbar accents 0.7 em below the baseline (our `lineTol` 0.5 em vs MuPDF 0.8 em), limits above `Σ` in display math.
* Positions comparator (6.10): `run.py --comparator positions` (`Tests/differential/positions.py`, `--selftest`, `--min-within P`), our glyph origins (`SAFEPDF_TEXT_GLYPHS=1`) vs `mutool draw -F stext` char origins converted back with MuPDF's page transform (via `mutool run`; fallback from our `pages` view); difflib alignment on normalized chars (ligature tails/U+FFFD not measured); tolerance max(1 pt, 0.1 × font size). Local: 766/768 pairs within (both misses = 6.11). Type 3 / `Tz` font-size fields differ from MuPDF (counted as `size_mismatch`, not position errors). `mutool run` can't read scripts from stdin. mutool on `Tests/adversarial/xobject-fanout.pdf` runs > 4 min / > 6 GB — positions treats a mutool timeout as skip.
* Raster limits (7.2d): `Limits.curveSegCap/coordLimit/maxDashSteps/arcSegCap/maxPageSide/clipCacheSize`, carried by `Fill.Config` (`Config.ofLimits l`, `cfg.segCap`, `cfg.arcCap`, `cfg.polyCap`); `Clip.StrokeFn` takes the `Config` first; `segCount cap m tol`, `ClipCache.insert cc cap c m`; all six degrade (no `LimitKind`): coarser flattening (`segCount_tol_of_cap` states when tol holds), clamp, solid stroke, coarser pen, page crop, cache only. `scripts/bench-raster.sh` (`leanc -O3`, no lakefile change): compiled 612×792 full-page fill 76–79 ms, 1000 glyph circles 36–38 ms, 119-line grid 67–71 ms, 1000 rects under a clip 21–23 ms; compositing (`Clip.compLoop`/`blendPx`) ≈ 150 ns/px dominates → 7.2e.
* Glyph rendering (7.3a): `Core.Font.Outline` (glyph-space segments, ≤ 65536 per glyph by type); `TTFont.glyphOutline gid`, `Font1.outline name` (Type 1 charstrings record path ops when asked; flex, `seac`), `CFFFont.outline gid` (all Type 2 path ops, flex, endchar accent); `Core.Raster.Glyph` — loads fonts with Text's keys (no Text edit), positions from `Text.layoutParts`, transform FontMatrix × [Tfs·Th 0 0 Tfs tx Trise] × Tm × CTM × view, one device path per run (≤ 2²⁰ segments), modes 0/4 fill, 1/5 stroke, 2/6 both, 3/7 nothing; outline cache 512/page; `renderPage page zoom rot ⟨fonts, sources⟩ items cfg` / `renderDocPage` render paths + text (use these, not `Clip.renderPaths`, for full pages); `runMasks` hook. Gaps: text clip modes 4–7 don't clip, non-embedded fonts draw nothing (no substitutes), Type 3 out of scope, CID-keyed CFF keeps only FontMatrix `a`, fonts parsed twice per page (Text + Glyph), a run filled at once (opposite-orientation overlapping glyphs could cancel). Suite `raster-glyph` (`SAFEPDF_PPM_DIR`). `open SafePdf.Core.Content` + `Raster.Fill` makes `Config`/`GState` ambiguous — qualify.
* Std-14 widths (6.11): a standard-14 font without `/Widths` gives codes with no glyph the substitute's `.notdef` advance = the font's `space` width (`Std14.notdefWidth`: Helvetica 278, Times/Symbol 250, Courier 600, ZapfDingbats 278), MuPDF `pdf_load_simple_font`/`ft_width`; `/MissingWidth` keeps precedence (MuPDF ignores it in that case — rare deviation). Width tables cover codes 0..255 only.
* Missing fonts (6.12): MuPDF's "hail mary" font for a `Tf` name that isn't in the resources / isn't a dict is non-embedded Times-Roman with StandardEncoding (`pdf_load_hail_mary_font`); `Text.FontInfo.unknown` now equals it (`hailMaryDict`, `buildFont` → `buildFontDict`). The adversarial encrypted fixtures had pages without `/Resources` — not a decryption bug. Deterministic encrypted fixtures: `qpdf --static-id --static-aes-iv --allow-weak-crypto --object-streams=disable --stream-data=preserve --encrypt "" owner 40|128|256 …`; generator `Tests/golden/generate_text_enc.py` (a `.py` can't live in `Tests/golden/text/`: every file there is a test input). Without CJK fonts, mutool prints a non-embedded CIDFontType2 as U+FFFD per byte even with ToUnicode — embed `/FontFile2` in test PDFs compared against MuPDF.
* Compositor (7.2e): `Clip.composite b W fm cm r rgb alpha` row-wise over `paintRect`, `UInt32` arithmetic, zero-coverage skip, opaque fast path; the old loop is `compLoopSpec`/`compositeSpec` (spec) and suite `raster-composite` checks byte equality on 5000 seeded random cases (with a guard that ≥ 1/3 of cases paint). Compiled 612×792: full fill 69 → 5.9 ms, grid 98 → 35 ms, circles 33 → 21 ms, clipped rects 20 → 11 ms; remaining time is `Fill.fillEdges` (7.2f). In compiled benches, pure work over closed constants is hoisted out of the timed region — pass data through a `@[noinline]` function argument.
* Images (7.3b): `Core.Raster.Image` — `loadImages limits src s res items` (each XObject decoded once per page into `ImageTable`), `decodePixmap` (filters, bpc 1/2/4/8/16 (16 → high byte), row padding, `/Decode`, `/ImageMask` with fill colour, colour spaces via `Run.csInfo`; DCT/JPX/JBIG2/CCITT, Pattern, unknown → mid-grey box), `w·h > maxImagePixels` → `limitExceeded .imagePixels`, `w·h·ncomp > maxDecodedStreamBytes` → `.decodedStreamBytes`, short data zero-padded (MuPDF); placement: inverse of CTM × view once per image, fixed-point stepping, nearest sample at device pixel centres, bbox ∩ clip window, compositing with clip mask and `ca`; local caps `maxPageImagePixels` 2²⁸, `maxComps` 32. Wired into `Glyph.renderItemG` (`PageFonts.images`, `renderDocPage` loads them). Suite `raster-image`. Compiled full-page 600×800 RGB: decode ≈ 52 ms, placement ≈ 97 ms. Gaps → 7.3e. Compiling single modules to C without `lake build`: `lake env lean -c out.c M.lean` per module, rename `lp_safe_x2dpdf_`→`l_` and `initialize_safe_x2dpdf_`→`initialize_`, link with `lake env leanc -O3 *.c`.
* Fast filler (7.2f): `Fill.fillEdges` uses an Int64 active-edge list with crossing order carried across sample rows (insertion-sort repair, budget 8n+64, else mergeSort) and one reused UInt32 accumulator; guarded (coords within ±2³⁰ subpixel units, windings −1..1, sane mask size), else the old filler `fillEdgesSpec`/`fillEdgesCountedSpec`; `ofContentPath` maps points with Int64 over a common denominator when safe (spec `ofContentPathSpec`). Suite `raster-fill-spec`: 4000 random paths + 1000 edge sets + 3000 mappings byte-equal (incl. counted steps). Compiled 612×792: grid 37 → 10.3 ms, 1000 discs 21 → 8.7 ms, full fill 4.5 ms. An edge not crossing the current sample row must be skipped in the sweep (bug the spec suite caught). Compiled `Int` above 2³¹ is a GMP bignum (even literals like `2^36`); `Array UInt32` cells are unboxed, `Array Int64` writes allocate. `Clip.fillEdgesCounted_w_le` unfolds `fillEdgesCounted` — keep it a plain structure literal. `perf` on this aarch64 sandbox: `apt install linux-perf`, run with `LD_PRELOAD=/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu/libtinfo.so.6`.
* Transparency (7.3c): `GState.BlendMode` (16 modes), `blendMode`/`softMask` in the graphics state; `FillStyle`/`StrokeStyle` gained `blend`/`smask` (write literals as `{ color := …, alpha := … }` — anonymous `⟨c, a⟩` no longer compiles); items `groupBegin g`/`groupEnd` (flat: text extraction sees text inside groups) and `softMask id info items` (nested: text/SVG skip mask contents); Run applies ExtGState `/BM` and `/SMask` (mask `/G` form under the same form fuel/visited/budget), transparency-group forms emit group items and reset alpha/blend/mask inside. `Core.Raster.Transparency`: 12 separable modes in integers, 4 non-separable in exact rationals, luminosity/alpha masks, page-size scratch layers (≤ 8 open, `maxLayerDepth` 8, `maxMasks` 8, `maxMaskDepth` 4); exact for group alpha and Normal masks; knockout and group `/CS`, `/TR` ignored; non-Normal blending exact only over opaque/empty content (Multiply, Screen, Exclusion exact everywhere). Wired: `Glyph.renderItems` folds through `Transparency.renderTx (painterG …)`. Combining scans the whole page (≈ 50 ms per group/mask) → 7.2g. Suite `raster-transparency`. A fuel-indexed driver calling `items.foldl (renderTx P W H f)` inside its `f+1` case is structural; a catch-all arm under a fuel match doesn't reduce for variable fuel — factor it out and `cases f`.
* Images (7.3e): `/SMask` (DeviceGray, own `/Decode`, nearest-resampled to the finer grid, `/Matte` un-premultiply), `/Mask` stencil streams and colour-key arrays, applied at decode time; MuPDF-like smoothing: axis-aligned images grid-fitted outward to whole pixels, per-axis box downscale when shrunk (MuPDF uses a tent filter and only when both axes shrink), bilinear for rotated/skewed and upscaled ≤ 2× (more only with `/Interpolate`), edge AA only for non-axis-aligned images (MuPDF has none — deliberate deviation); `place … (smooth := false)` = old nearest mode. Compiled: decode 600×800 RGB 52 → 6 ms, full-page placement 100 → 20 ms. `UInt8.ofNatClamp` ≈ 30 ns/call compiled — use `Image.u8` (`if n < 256 then UInt8.ofNat n else 255`, `u8_eq`). `opaque`/`partial` are keywords. `lake env lean -c` only accepts files inside the project root.
* Shadings (7.3d): `Core.Raster.Shading` — `Fn.eval` for function types 0 (multilinear, ≤ 8 inputs, ≤ 2²⁰ samples), 2 (exact for integer N, fixed-point log/exp otherwise), 3 (depth ≤ 8, ≤ 256 nodes), 4 (full bounded PostScript calculator: 64 KiB, nesting 100, stack 100; error → zeros), exact rationals rounded to 2⁻²⁴; axial/radial via a 256-entry colour LUT per shading (MuPDF), `/Extend`, larger valid radial root (Newton seeded from the previous pixel), no edge AA; types 1, 4–7 as Gouraud triangles (type 1 65×65 grid, patches 8×8; caps 2¹⁶ vertices, 4096 patches, 2²⁰ triangles/page, 2²⁶ pixel visits/item); `/BBox` as extra clip, `/Background` only for patterns; `renderFill` paints `.pattern n` fills with PatternType 2 (tiling → 7.3g). Wired into `Glyph.renderItemG` (`PageFonts.shadings`, `loadShadings` in `renderDocPage`). Compiled 612×792: axial 4–5 ms, radial 6–7 ms, function-based mesh 30 ms. Suite `raster-shading`. Gaps → 7.3f. Generic `Int`/`Nat` per-pixel loops ≈ 200 ns/px compiled; `Int64` + `Clip.pxU` < 10 ns/px; `Int64.toNatClampNeg` (no `Int64.toNat`); `pgrep -f "lake build"` matches the waiting shell itself.
* Tiling (7.3g): `Core.Raster.Tiling` — `loadTiling`/`loadTilings` run the cell through `Run.runOps` (pattern resources, shared op/form `Budget`, frame `⟨res, [num], num, 0, Matrix.identity⟩`); cell rendered over white and black (gives colour + coverage exactly: source-over is affine in the backdrop); small cells (≤ 2²⁰ px) rendered once and blitted at whole-pixel tile offsets (MuPDF `fz_draw_begin_tile`/`end_tile`), no AA `/BBox` clip (MuPDF; AA clipping leaves seams); larger cells rendered per tile (≤ 64) else grey; caps `maxCellPixels` 2²⁰, `maxTiles` 2²⁰, `maxTileWork` 2²⁷, `maxDirectTiles` 64; `pathPainter` paints paths only. NOT wired into the renderer yet (7.3h). Compiled full-page small-cell fills 12–30 ms. Suite `raster-tiling`. `Fixed` test values via `Fixed.clamp raw`.
* Display-list gaps (7.3f): `ResRef` (`.page`, `.ref num gen`, `.form num gen`) on `sh` items and in `PatternUse {res, base, under}` (`FillStyle.pattern`/`StrokeStyle.pattern : Option PatternUse`, name stays in `Color.pattern n`); `Frame` gained `ownerGen`, `base`; `Run.resLookup src s pageRes "Pattern"|"Shading" res name`; `Shading.patternRef c u`; effective pattern matrix `Matrix.mul patMatrix base`; inline images' named `/ColorSpace` substituted from the current frame (`CSInfo.toObj`); loaders walk soft-mask items (`DisplayList.foldDeep`); `sh` items carry a `FillStyle` (alpha painted directly; blend/mask via a one-item group `Glyph.liftSh`); shading-pattern strokes (`Shading.renderStroke`) and text. Under colour recorded at `SCN`/`scn`, not saved by `q`/`Q` (matched by pattern name). Adding a `Machine` field breaks anonymous constructors/patterns in `Interp.paintStep`, `Spec.Content` `Effect.paint`/`paintStep_eq`, `Run.formMachine`.
* Layer speed (7.2g): `Transparency` layers track dirty rects and combine only over them; single-item blend/mask layers reuse a spare buffer over `Painter.bound` (= `Clip.itemBound`, enabled in `Glyph.painterG`); spec driver `renderTxSpec`, suite `raster-tx-fast` (3000 combine cases + 1500 random scenes byte-equal). Compiled: 100 groups 1586 → 9 ms, 1000 Multiply rects 61.7 s → 12 ms, luminosity-masked fill 62 → 9 ms. `Clip.u8` (= `UInt8.ofNatClamp`, `u8_eq`) used by `blend`/`Mask.inter`. ByteArrays are written in place only if unshared: take the tx state out (`{}`) before writing backdrop/spare buffers; never re-read a buffer after passing it to `ByteArray.copySlice`. Overlay/SoftLight are neutral at byte 128, HardLight isn't.
* Raster view + pixels (7.4): `inspect --view raster [--json] FILE` (`SAFEPDF_PAGE`, `SAFEPDF_DPI` default 72, `SAFEPDF_RASTER_DIR`, `SAFEPDF_RASTER_FORMAT=png`), per-page compiled render time in the JSON, fresh decode budget per page; `run.py --comparator pixels [--dpi D] [--exclude GLOB] [--min-within P]` (`pixels.py --selftest`): reference `mutool draw -s t -r D -c rgb -F pnm`; a pixel differs if any channel is > 32/255 outside the other image's 3×3 neighbourhood range (both ways); a page is within if ≤ 1% of pixels differ (correct text pages 0.28–0.32%, missing text 8–9%). Local: every page we render is within; pdfTeX pages 21/23 within (Type 3 bitmap fonts draw nothing); our median text page 205 ms vs MuPDF 1 ms → 7.2h; inspector start-up ≈ 1 s → 7.5. `run.py --files` globs are recursive. `gdb -p PID -batch -ex "thread apply all bt 60"` samples work (perf is broken here; Lean work runs on a separate thread). Fixture generator `Tests/differential/fixtures/gen_tex_pages.py`; docs `docs/raster.md`; CI step in `docs/ci-pending.patch` (was `docs/ci-pixels-step.patch`) (not applied: token lacks `workflow` scope).
* Start-up (7.5): Lean evaluates every closed term (`def x : T := …` without arguments) of every imported module at program start — test-only constants used by `#guard` must be functions `(_ : Unit) → T` called as `(x ())`. LZW's five test constants cost ≈ 1.2 s; `inspect --help` now 6 ms. `/usr/bin/time` is not installed; use the shell's `time`.
* Annotations (8.3): `Core.Annot` — `collect` (page `/Annots` ≤ `maxAnnots` 4096, dedup refs, skip Link/Popup/Invisible/Hidden/NoView, Widgets need `/FT` + `/T` (≤ 32 `/Parent` levels), `/AP /N` stream or `/AS` entry, MuPDF order: non-widgets then widgets), matrix A §12.5.5 (`aOf`, exact ℚ then rounded; `aOf_corners`), each appearance run via `Run.formJob` + `Run.runOps` with `/BBox` clip, group, own resources (fallback page); `docAnnots limits doc page pageFonts bud budget : AnnotPage × Budget` (`items`, extended `fonts`, `roots`, drawn/skipped, warnings); wired in `Glyph.renderDocPage` (items appended after page content; own op/form budget — 8.3b). No appearance synthesis yet. Run appearances with `formJob`, not a synthetic `Do` (`xobjectOf` needs `/Subtype /Form`). Suite `annot`; vs mutool 2/40000 px differ.
* Stroker (7.2i): pen offsets A/B and unit tangents rounded once to a 2⁻¹⁶ grid (`gridQ`, `gridQ_err` ≤ 2⁻¹⁷ subpixel units), then Int64 for points/miter tests/tips/orientation when `SegG.small`, exact Int otherwise; Rat spec `strokeEdgesSpec`/`strokeDPathSpec`/`mkSegSpec` includes the same grid rounding; suite `raster-stroke-spec` (8000 cases byte-equal). Vector page 123 → 30 ms compiled. `Int64 >>>` floors; `(n + 2³¹) >>> 32` rounds n/2³² half up; `roundDivI` needs no Rat normalisation.
* Glyph speed (7.2h): per-run Int64 linear part (`GXf.ofAff?`) + per-glyph translation (`GTx`), each point rounded with a proven error bound, exact `Rat` fallback near rounding ties / out of range (so output = spec `runPathSpec`); `columnGroups` splits a run's fill into column-disjoint subpath groups (closed contours have net winding 0 per sample row ⇒ identical coverage); font matrices computed once per source. Suite `raster-glyph-spec` (3000 outlines incl. 600 ties, 150 runs, group-sum check). pdfTeX text pages 205 → 44–47 ms compiled; remaining ≈ 60% in `Fill.fillEdgesCounted` (7.2j); fonts parsed twice per page (Text + Glyph). Compiled code rebuilds Nat literals > 2³² from strings on every call (`lean_cstr_to_nat`) — compare with Int64 literals. `pkill -f` can kill your own shell; use `pkill -x`.
* Filler (7.2j): small masks (each glyph after `columnGroups`) use a bucketed route — each edge's sample-row crossings computed once, sorted by row, swept (≤ 2²⁰ sample rows/px wide/crossings, ≤ 64 crossings per row); the active-edge route orders edges by first row with a counting sort, `sortAct` allocation-free, finished edges dropped in place, crossings updated in place; cubic vertices by exact forward differences (`cubicFD_eq`). Text page 45 → 25 ms, vector 32 → 26 ms, image 20 → 16 ms compiled (byte-identical); remaining text-page time: text extraction ≈ 35%, `Glyph.runPath` ≈ 25%, fill+flatten ≈ 25%; `IAff.ofAff?` computes `Nat.lcm` with GMP per path. Build a structure update at exactly one constructor site, or compiled code frees and reallocates instead of updating in place; functions returning pairs allocate per call. `gen_tex_pages.py` must be run so that pdflatex works inside OUTDIR (it calls `pdflatex` in that directory; check the PDFs exist).
* Viewer (8.2a): `lakefile.lean` replaces `lakefile.toml` (same libs/exes/options; lib `SafePdfApp` holds `SafePdf.Shell.App.Tcl`/`TclLemmas` so the axioms audit sees them after `lake build`); Tk shim is a custom `target tkShim` attached only to `lean_exe viewer` (not default; an `extern_lib` would be linked into every exe), compile flags via `pkg-config tk` at shim build time, link flags computed at lakefile load (empty if Tk missing — run `lake -R` after installing Tk); `c/tk_shim.c` (interp, Tk, `::safepdf::emit` callback, `safepdf_tk_photo_put` RGBA → photo via `Tk_PhotoPutBlock`); `SafePdf/Shell/App/` (`Tcl.lean` + `TclLemmas.lean` copied from bib-lean with credit — proved `Tcl.quote`; `Shim.lean`, `Viewer.lean`, `Main.lean`): open via argv or `tk_getOpenFile`/Ctrl+O, fit-to-window render with `Glyph.renderDocPage`, re-render on resize, next/prev/Home/End, title `NAME - page k/n - safe-pdf`, errors in a status line; `scripts/viewer-smoke.sh [--no-build]` under Xvfb (non-white pixels, key navigation, a hostile file name verbatim in the title, bad files don't crash). `docs/viewer.md`. ubuntu-24.04 `tk-dev` is Tk 8.6 (shim syntax-checked against 8.6.16 headers only). Tk `photo write -format ppm` fails on an empty photo.
* 7.3h stalled (wave 34): the agent stopped making progress for 90 min mid-rewrite of `renderPage`/`renderDocPage`; its partial diff (Glyph/Tiling/tests, 784 lines, unbuilt) is saved as `docs/wip-7.3h-tiling-wiring.patch` — the next 7.3h attempt may start from it but must re-verify everything; consider splitting 7.3h (fills only first, then strokes/text, then full cell painter).
* Tiling wiring (7.3h1): `Tiling.TilingTable` keyed by `(ResRef × Name)` (`patternKey`, `underOf` — black without `scn` operands, `tileOf` → pattern, `Aff.ofMatrix (Matrix.mul pat.matrix use.base)`, PaintType-2 colour); `loadTilings` walks `foldDeep` items and resolves via `Run.resLookup`; `Glyph.PageFonts.tilings`; `renderItemG` sends tiling-pattern `.fillPath` items to `Tiling.renderTilingFill` with `pathPainter` (strokes/text/full cells: 7.3h2); cells budgeted with what page + annotations left. Fixtures `Tests/golden/raster-tiling/*.pdf` (suite fails if regenerated bytes differ; `SAFEPDF_GOLDEN_UPDATE=1`); 0.000% differing pixels vs mutool at 72 dpi. Run `lake build` before the `pixels` comparator (`lake build tests` doesn't rebuild `inspect`). Tiling cells start at the pattern-space origin. Build `PageFonts` with field syntax `{ fonts := …, sources := … }`.
* Viewer (8.2b): pure view logic in `SafePdf/Shell/App/View.lean` (Tk-free, ~40 `#guard`s, `reanchor_self`; in lib `SafePdfApp`) — zoom fit page/fit width/25–800 % steps (100 % = 72 dpi × `tk scaling`), anchor kept via `toPage`/`toScreen`, rotation via `rotAdd`, page-at-a-time scrolling across page boundaries, LRU cache 256 MiB / 64 entries (images > half the budget uncached), next-page prefetch after 100 ms if ≤ ~4 Mpx, per-image cap 2²⁶ px ("zoom limited"), crop message at `maxPageSide`. Tk: leave `-x/yscrollincrement` at default (non-default increments round `moveto`); `tk scaling` under Xvfb ≈ 1.39 — keep the stored zoom percentage; `<Key-plus>` = `<Key-+>`; smoke mode must create its output dir first. Letter at 800 dpi renders in 509 ms.
* Annotations (8.3b): `Text.PageText.ops/forms` (page run counts); `Annot.Budget.after limits pt` (`docAnnots_shared_ops`: page + annotation ops ≤ `maxOpsPerPage`); `Annot.extractDocPageAnnots` (page then annotation glyphs, item indices continue; the `text` view uses it); synthesized appearances (MuPDF 1.25.1 `pdf-appearance.c`) for Square, Circle (incl. MuPDF's `rd.x1` bottom quirk), Line (leader lines), Ink, Highlight (Multiply), Underline, StrikeOut — `/BBox` = recomputed rect, identity matrix, rect replaced; `Drawable.synth`; 0.15% differing px vs mutool on the 9-annotation fixture. `Glyph.loadSources … (owner := r.1)` for appearance resources. Gaps → 8.3c. MuPDF draws `b` with alpha < 1 as a knockout group; we blend the stroke over the fill (≈ 2% diff).
* Tiling complete (7.3h2): tiling-pattern strokes (`Tiling.renderTilingStroke`) and text (`Tiling.paintTilingMask`, runs not column-split); `Glyph.renderItemWith` parametrised by a cell painter; `cellPainterD d` paints cells with the full renderer (text with fonts from the pattern's own resources, images, shadings, soft masks), nested patterns at depth d−1, depth 0 = paths-only (nested → mid grey); `Tiling.maxTileDepth` 2; `loadTilingsWith` loads patterns named by fills/strokes/glyph runs and those inside loaded cells for `maxTileDepth + 1` levels, each once (self-reference terminates); 9 fixtures in `Tests/golden/raster-tiling/` within 1% vs mutool. Cell graphics state starts from defaults (not the parent's). A depth-recursive painter inside `renderItemG` makes `simp [painterG]` overflow — use `show (Clip.whiteBuf _ _).size = _`.
* Viewer selection/search (8.2c): pure logic in `SafePdf/Shell/App/Select.lean` (lib `SafePdfApp`): reading-order glyph list (full text = `Text.layout`'s page text, `#guard`), glyph boxes (fixed 0.2 em below / 0.8 em above the baseline), nearest glyph, drag range, word, copy text, highlight boxes, search with `findAll_sound`; drag-select with translucent canvas overlays (outlines on macOS), Ctrl+C / right-click menu → clipboard via `Tcl.quote`, Ctrl+A, double-click word; Ctrl+F find bar (own `bindtags` so typed keys don't trigger viewer shortcuts), Enter/F3, Shift+F3, Escape; case-insensitive (ASCII + Latin-1), whitespace-normalised, page by page with wrap, per-page lazy text cache; status `"q": hit k/n on page p` (n = hits on that page). Smoke test checks clipboard text vs the inspector's text and highlight position vs a drawn marker. Tk: `event generate <ButtonPress-1>` twice = `<Double-Button-1>`; `<Motion> -state 256` fires `<B1-Motion>`.
* Annotations (8.3c): `/OC` per MuPDF `pdf_read_ocg`/`pdf_is_ocg_hidden_imp` (`/Configs [0]` wins over `/D`, BaseState/ON/OFF/Intent/Usage ViewState; OCMD arrays `/VE` visible; `/P` uses MuPDF's arithmetic — `AllOn` visible if any on, `AnyOff` hidden if any on — confirmed vs mutool) → `Skip.ocHidden`; `readOC` in `docAnnots`; synthesized `/LE` (9 endings), Polygon/PolyLine, Squiggly, Caret, Text/FileAttachment/Sound icons (14 open-iconic paths from MuPDF `annotation-icons.h`); 17-annotation fixture differs from mutool in 22/90,000 px. MuPDF NoZoom is a TODO in 1.25.1; NoRotate undoes only the page `/Rotate` about (x0, y1). Quick suite loop: `lake env lean --run Scratch.lean` importing `Tests.Annot` after `lake build tests`.
* Viewer links (8.2d): pure logic in `SafePdf/Shell/App/Links.lean` (lib `SafePdfApp`; `uriText_ascii` — displayed URIs are printable ASCII with percent-encoding; `History.back_push`); outline panel (`ttk::treeview` in a `ttk::panedwindow`, F9, titles cleaned of controls/bidi overrides, ≤ 500 chars), destinations scroll via C2 (viewer keeps its zoom; `/XYZ` zoom ignored); link hover cursor + status target; internal/named links jump; URI links never opened — confirmation dialog shows the exact URI, copies it to the clipboard on confirm; Launch/GoToR/JavaScript reported "not followed"; Alt+Left/Right history (links, outline, Ctrl+G only); page labels "page iii (3/120)"; Ctrl+G: exact label → number → case-insensitive label. `Nav.Target.action` drops the `/URI` string — the shell reads `/A /URI` itself (Core follow-up). v4.36 has no `Char.toNat_ofNat`; PDFDocEncoding 0x80 = bullet. A Tk `vwait` modal works inside an `emit` handler.
* Library API (8.4): `SafePdf/Api.lean` (pure facade; `openFile` the only IO) — `openBytes` (`OpenOptions {limits, allowRepair}`), `Document.pageCount/repaired/encrypted/version`, `pageInfo`, `pageText` (reading-order text, lines with boxes, glyphs with Unicode/origin/box/size/dir/provenance; `withAnnots`), `renderPage d i zoom rot` (RGBA), `toScreen`/`toPage`, `outline`, `pageLinks` (URIs kept), `pageAnnotations`, `textString`, `Api.Error`; `open` is a keyword (hence `openBytes`); theorems `openBytes_strict`, C2 `toPage_toScreen`/`toScreen_toPage`, `toScreen_eq`, C6 `pageText_glyphs` (page content only), `glyph_provenance`/`glyph_codes` (C3), `renderPage_size`, `outline_bounded`; `lean_lib SafePdfCore` (root `SafePdf.Api`, default target) + `lean_exe extract` (`examples/Extract.lean`, `lake exe extract [--render] FILE [PAGE]`); `docs/api.md`; hygiene's strict scan now covers `SafePdf/Api.lean`; umbrella imports it. Inside `def X.f`, a bare `none` pattern may resolve to `X.none` — write `Option.none`.
* Annotations (8.3d): NoRotate per MuPDF (post-multiply A by the page `/Rotate` about (x0, y1); pixel-identical at 0/90/180/270), synthesized FreeText (`/DA`, `/Q`, wrapping, `/RD`, border, `/Rotate` 90/180/270, callouts with `/LE`; non-embedded base-14 font resource — text in extraction, not painted), Stamp (MuPDF stamp texts, Times-Bold, 0.6° turn, 190×50 box), Line `/Cap` (inline or above); synthesized forms get pseudo object numbers `synthBase + i` (2⁴⁰) as font owners. Gaps → 8.3e. Bytes ≥ 128 in synthesized content: octal escapes (content passes through `String.toUTF8`). `winAnsiNames` maps undefined cp1252 codes to `bullet` — exclude when inverting. `lake exe tests NAME` ignores the argument.
* Agent supervision (wave 37): a worktree with no file changes for 30 min is not proof of a stall — 7.3i's last message before it was stopped was "Now fixture PDFs" (it was mid-task). Judge activity by the growth of the agent's transcript file size (`stat -c %s <output_file>`, never read it) before stopping an agent.
* Knockout emission (7.3i1): `Paint.items` emits `groupBegin {knockout, non-isolated, blend, smask}` + fill + stroke (Normal, unmasked) + `groupEnd` for `b`/`B` unless stroke alpha 0 or (alpha 1 and Normal) — the group carries the blend mode and soft mask (MuPDF `pdf_begin_group`), else Transparency's alpha-1 knockout fast path blends twice; text needs no interpreter group (7.3i2's renderer already knocks out `2 Tr` runs via `Transparency.splitRun?`); `Paint.maxItems` 4; spec `paintItems` + `paintItems_eq`. Fixtures `Tests/differential/fixtures/knockout/` (`gen_knockout.py`), all within 1% vs mutool (without the group three fail by 9–10%).
* Nav URIs (8.2e): `Nav.Target.uri (bytes : List UInt8)` for `/S /URI` with a string `/URI` ≤ `maxUriBytes` 8192 (longer → bare `.action "URI"`, never truncated); used by `Api.Target.ofCore` and the viewer (`Links.actionOf`).
* FreeText/cloudy (8.3e): `/AcroForm /DA` fallback (carried as `OCDesc.acroDA`); rich text per MuPDF 1.25.1 with its HTML engine: `/RC` or `/DS`+`/Contents` → rich layout, black default text, size from `/DA`, first baseline 0.9 × size, lines 1.2 × size, from x = 2b to w − 4b, `/Q` ignored for CSS `text-align`; `/RC` reduced to text (paragraph/`<br>` breaks; inline styles ignored); cloudy borders `/BE` for Square/Circle/Polygon ported from MuPDF (exact-rational trig ≈ 10⁻¹¹; `maxCloudPts` 4096; FreeText cloudy = plain border as in MuPDF). `mutool run` + `annot.update()` dumps MuPDF's synthesized `/AP` for exact stream comparison.
* Type 3 rendering (7.3j): `Core.Raster.Type3` — fonts loaded once per page (also from annotations and tiling cells), charprocs run once per (font, code) into glyph space (shared glyph names run once); `d0` keeps colours, `d1` (or neither) = mask in the run's fill colour (glyph colour operators ignored); bounds from drawn content → `d1` box → `/FontBBox` → window (MuPDF); per-instance matrix FontMatrix × [Tfs·Th 0 0 Tfs tx Trise] × Tm × CTM × view through the surrounding painter (nested Type 3 share the tiling depth cap 2); glyph origins snapped like MuPDF `fz_subpixel_adjust` (whole px across the baseline from 8 px, quarter px along it below 24 px); stencil-only glyphs drawn with an exact area-weighted mask and no grid fitting (MuPDF disables image grid fitting in Type 3: `FZ_DRAWDEV_FLAGS_TYPE3`); caps `maxGlyphOps` 100000, 256 glyphs/font, `maxType3Fonts` 64, `maxGlyphLayerPixels` 2²², `maxStencilSide` 4096. pdfTeX type3 page 0.977%/0.866% vs mutool (was blank), ~57 ms/page. Gaps: modes 1/2/4–6 stroke/clip, pattern fills on masked glyphs, fonts/patterns inside charprocs, no bitmap cache → 7.3k. `Tiling.cellLayer` + `Tiling.blit` renders any item list offscreen with recovered alpha.
* JPEG (7.6a): `SafePdf/Quarantine/DCT.lean` (strict hygiene) — baseline/extended-sequential 8-bit Huffman, 1–4 components, restart intervals, multi-scan non-interleaved, libjpeg islow IDCT, libjpeg-turbo 2.1 fancy upsampling, integer YCbCr→RGB / YCCK→CMYK; colour rule as MuPDF (Adobe APP14 overrides `/ColorTransform`, 0 = none, CMYK not inverted for PDF JPEGs); progressive/arithmetic/12-bit/DNL/fractional sampling → `Error.unsupported` (placeholder); caps `maxImagePixels`, planes/output ≤ `maxDecodedStreamBytes`, one scan per component; `decode_pixels`/`finalize_pixels`. Image.lean: byte filters before `/DCTDecode` go through `src.decode` (budget charged), result revalidated (component count vs colour space; missing `/ColorSpace` from the JPEG), read as 8-bit raster with `/Decode`; XObjects, inline images, DCT soft masks. Fixtures `Tests/images/` (`gen_jpegs.py`, 13 JPEG/`.ref` pairs, bit-exact vs `djpeg -dct int`); suite `dct`; 1000² 4:2:0 ≈ 110 ms compiled; 15/15 PDFs pixel-identical to mutool. Debian's mutool links system libjpeg-turbo 2.1.5 (not MuPDF's bundled IJG 9) — `djpeg -dct int` is the exact reference here. Compile scratch programs under `.lake/bench/` and link `.lake/build/lib/libsafe_x2dpdf_{Tests,SafePdf}.a` after `lake build SafePdf:static Tests:static`.
* Progressive JPEG (7.6b): SOF2 — all coefficients kept (libjpeg 16-bit wrapping) until EOI, then dequantised (tables fixed at a component's first scan) and IDCT'd via `St.flush`; DC first/refine, AC first (zero runs, EOB runs)/refine per libjpeg-turbo `jdphuff.c`; scan headers checked like libjpeg (bad → malformed; odd scan orders decoded as they come); running out of data leaves the rest of the restart interval unchanged; restarts reset DC predictions and EOB run; coefficients charged 8 bytes each against `maxDecodedStreamBytes` (≈ 22 Mpx 4:2:0), `maxScans` 256. 12 progressive fixtures (`cjpeg -progressive`, `-scans` refinement scripts, `jpegtran -progressive` CMYK) bit-exact vs `djpeg -dct int`; 25/25 fixture PDFs pixel-identical to mutool; 1000² progressive 4:2:0 164 ms compiled (baseline 124 ms). Not reproduced: libjpeg block smoothing for incomplete progressive data (→ 7.6c). `djpeg -nosmooth` disables fancy upsampling, not block smoothing. Clear a coefficient array held in a structure (`{ st with coef := #[] }`) before mutating it, or it's copied per scan.
* Type 3 scaling (7.3k): MuPDF's stencil scaler ported for downscaled Type 3 glyph images (`l2factor` power-of-two subsampling incl. stencil inversion and `/Decode [1 0]`, smoothstep weights as `make_weights`/`check_weights`, two-pass byte rounding; `weightsAt` exact ℚ reference, `weightsAtI` fixed point); Type 3 advances truncated to 1/1000 em for rendering (`truncWidth`, MuPDF `pdf_add_hmtx`); per-run mask cache (≤ 1024, whole-pixel moves exact) + per-font lazy subsampled bitmaps (`Thunk` in the loaded font). pdfTeX type3 page 0.000%/0.000% vs mutool (max channel diff 4, compositing rounding); 56–59 ms/page. In Lean `>>>` binds tighter than `*`. Compiled `Nat` stays a machine word to 2⁶³ (Int only to 2³¹).
* Type 3 follow-ups (7.3l): page-wide glyph-mask cache `Glyph.TState.t3 : Type3.GlyphCache` (key font uid/code/scale/phase; ≤ 4096 entries, ≤ 16 MiB; `insert_count_le`/`insert_bytes_le`; each tiling cell has its own): pdfTeX type3 page 60 → 40 ms (72 dpi), 94 → 49 ms (150 dpi); `Text.type3TruncWidth` (MuPDF `pdf_add_hmtx`, toward zero, 1/1000 em) used by both text and rendering — positions vs MuPDF on the type3 page 0.156 → 0.000 pt median; Type 3 render modes per MuPDF (`fz_render_stroked_glyph` falls back to fill for non-FreeType fonts; fill-then-stroke for 2/6; 3/7 nothing; clip effect of 4–7 not implemented; no knockout for 2/6). `mutool draw` writes grey PNM by default — pass `-c rgb`. `inspect --view raster` overwrites `page-N.ppm` per directory.
* JPEG follow-ups (7.6c): standard Huffman tables only for sequential frames (libjpeg-turbo `jinit_huff_decoder`; progressive without DHT stays an error); libjpeg-turbo 2.1.5 `jdcoefct.c` block smoothing (incl. block-row edge quirks) — truncated/DC-only progressive fixtures bit-exact; truncated baseline: rest of image grey 128, scan stops at once without restarts (cut 4096² in 139 ms); truncated table segments read like libjpeg. References now from `JSIMD_FORCENONE=1 djpeg -dct int` (C code); mutool uses SIMD, which differs on overflowing blocks (→ 7.6d). djpeg exits 2 on truncated files but still writes the image. libjpeg smooths whenever any of AC coefficients 1–9 was never scanned.

* Glyph names `uniXXXXXXXX` (8+ hex digits) are NOT ligatures for MuPDF: it reads `uni` only in exactly 7-char names; longer → U+FFFD, then Type 3 maps codes 32..126 to ASCII (6.8a).
* Figure text can live in a form XObject's own `/Resources`; `mutool info -F` on the page does not list those fonts (6.8a).
* `mutool draw -F txt/stext` clips text to the page box per glyph (`FZ_STEXT_CLIP`; "Edge" → "Edg"); Core.Text does the same via `Options.pageBox` / `Options.forPage` (6.8b).
* MuPDF replaces glyphs inside `/ActualText` spans by the ActualText chars at those glyphs' positions; difflib pairs runs of identical chars leftmost, so one extra leading char shows as a constant pitch offset, not a position bug (6.8b).
* Substitute fonts (7.7): `Shell/Fonts.lean` searches `SAFEPDF_FONT_DIR`, URW base-35, TeX Gyre; `SAFEPDF_SUBST_FONTS=0` disables; Core `Raster/Subst.lean` follows `pdf-font.c` (exact alias table, spaces ignored, no subset-prefix strip; no descriptor → Times-Roman; /Flags for the rest; no /FontWeight/StemV in 1.25.1); MuPDF stretches glyphs to `/Widths` (integer, truncated) only for substituted fonts, never for standard names/aliases; `renderDocPage … (subst := …)` defaults to empty. Non-embedded CID fonts still unpainted.
* `scripts/viewer-smoke.sh` text-mode case runs with `SAFEPDF_SUBST_FONTS=0` (it assumed invisible Helvetica) (7.7).
* Worktree guard refuses Bash commands combining a raw.githubusercontent.com URL with loops/other commands: one plain `curl` per command (6.8a, 6.8b).
* Corpus pixels (M7) on 4d21815+merges, before 7.7: 88.08% of 2,450 pages within, median 60 ms (p95 2.6 s, max 42 s); 7.7 took 10 of the 11 worst files to 100%.
* Ghostscript Type 3 bitmap glyphs are inline CCITT G4 masks (`/IM true /F /CCF /D [1 0]`) inside Flate-compressed CharProcs — `grep /CCF` on the file finds nothing; `mutool show -b FILE N`. A `d1` glyph whose image codec is missing paints a solid box (placeholder takes the fill colour) (7.8).
* CCITT (7.8): MuPDF fax decoder skips leading zero bits, emits the partial row on a bad code; `/EncodedByteAlign` skips `(12 − pos) mod 8` bits with `/EndOfLine`, else `(8 − pos) mod 8`. `/CCF` in an image's `/Mask` stream (`loadMask`) is still unmasked.
* MuPDF 1.25.1 maps Type 3 glyph names `/1`..`/9` to U+0001..U+0009 (digits vanish); check `pdftotext` before blaming our text. Known reference defects live in `Tests/differential/known-reference-defects.json` (SHA-256 + pages + signature regex that must still match MuPDF's text, else `STALE`); `run.py --no-known` gives raw numbers (6.8c).
* difflib/LCS alignment pairs single chars from unrelated text and runs of equal chars leftmost — both look like huge position errors; `positions.py` now anchors short blocks to neighbouring long blocks and re-pairs identical runs ≤ 2 slots (6.8c).
* Tk 9 canvas: `yview moveto` rounds to the nearest pixel (use `p/region`); no `yview scroll N pixels` — smooth scrolling uses `moveto`; `event generate <Button-5>` still fires Button-5 bindings; default `wish` here is 8.6 while the viewer links Tk 9.0.1 — use `tclsh9.0` for experiments (9.2).
* Viewer commands live once in `Shell/App/Menu.lean` (label, accelerator, Tcl script, enabled rule); menubar and toolbar are built from it — 9.7 rebuilds the toolbar on the same table (9.1).
* ActualText (6.8d): `BDC` with `/ActualText` (inline or `/Properties` name) → display items `actualTextBegin`/`actualTextEnd`; `Text.shownItems` / `PageText.shown` apply it (lines, text, inspector glyphs use `shown`; `glyphs` stays raw; `shownItems_eq_extract` when no ActualText); `Limits.maxMarkedDepth` 256; MuPDF fake-bold dedupe needs the same code bytes. A new def before `Interp.simple` renumbers its matcher (`simple_match_fallthrough` names it).
* Writer (9.5a): linearized files' `startxref` points at the first-page section near the start, so an update's `/Prev` is small — correct; `split at h` on a def with `let` before an `if` needs `dsimp only at h` first; worktree Bash refuses compound commands mentioning `git`.
* Tk: a widget packed `-in` a later-created sibling frame must be `raise`d; nested outline tree = one elide tag per ancestor; elided text rows return an empty `bbox` (visibility test); pdf.js `pdf_sidebar.js` is now `web/sidebar.js` (9.7).
* Importing `Lean.Data.Json` into an executable links the whole Lean library (+130 MB); `Std.Async.TCP` adds ~0.5 MB; `Std.Http` in v4.36 is server-only (10.1). `mutual` fuel recursion needs `termination_by structural fuel => fuel`; `IO.FS.createTempDir` honours `TMPDIR`.
* Tk 9 `photo get X Y` returns "R G B" (pixel checks in smoke); a Tk menu has no `entrycget -type` — use `$menu type INDEX`; an exception inside a smoke step is swallowed by `guarded` and the run times out after 60 s — wrap new steps in try/catch and destroy `.`; `xdotool` is installed (9.4).
* Printer writes whole-number reals as `2.`; `decide` cannot separate `Name.ofString` literals (use byte lists + a `#guard`); `omega` hits the recursion limit on nested `% 1024 / 256 % 256` terms — factor a one-variable lemma (9.5b).
* Tk `event generate` clicks in quick succession on one widget can register as a double click — pass `-time` and space clicks with `after 600`; `lake build` never compiles un-rooted Shell modules (add them to `SafePdfApp` roots); v4.36 core lacks `Rat.le_max_left`/`Rat.min_le_right` — `rw [Rat.max_def, Rat.min_def]`, split, `grind`; the worktree guard refuses large heredoc Python edits — write the script to the scratchpad (9.8).
* MuPDF text: pen excludes `Tc`/`Tw`, thresholds in units of the glyph being added; text outside the clip paths' bbox is dropped; `FontInfo.width` is costly — derive `Glyph.adv` from the advance already computed (6.8e/f).
* Writer prints strings in hex (`/NM <7361…>`), so byte-grep for `(safepdf-mark-` finds nothing; `pkill -f PATTERN` inside a Bash call kills the calling shell; under Xvfb without a WM `xdotool key` reaches nothing after a toplevel popup closes; a Tk binding that destroys its own widget needs `break`; wall clock: `Std.Time.Timestamp.now` (import `Std.Time.DateTime.Timestamp`) (9.5c).
* An encrypted aux stream's `/Length` is the ciphertext length; qpdf R6 (AES-256) output is not reproducible even with `--static-id --static-aes-iv`; pixel probes in page coordinates break on `/Rotate` and coloured paper — compare against the original render (9.5f).
* Perf (7.9): `perf` works only with `sudo env LD_PRELOAD=/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu/libtinfo.so.6 perf …`; a `let tbl := …; fun x => …` inside a function-typed `match` is eta-expanded and the table recomputed per call — keep tables as data; `setB (setB d …) … (getB dest …)` copies the ByteArray on each write when `dest` is read after the first write; ByteArray appends from `.empty` reallocate — reserve with `emptyWithCapacity`; `hardLight` layers combine over the whole page; the inspector's stdout is block-buffered (pages lost when killed).
* `set -e` + `[ … ] && grep …` ends a test script when grep fails before `check $?` runs (`launch-test.sh` has it latent); `IO.Process.exit` after the window closes avoids waiting on a dedicated thread stuck in a post; viewer-smoke failed once with `out/state.txt` missing right after a build, then passed twice — watch for flakiness (10.3).
* `DISPLAY=:99` in this sandbox is a stale socket — run the viewer smoke with `env -u DISPLAY` so it uses `xvfb-run` (explains the flaky `out/state.txt` failures); Tk calls `-yscrollcommand` at idle, so scrolls the viewer makes itself must call `viewChanged` directly; no `/usr/bin/time` here — sample VmHWM from /proc (9.3).
* bib-lean POST replaces the whole annotation set — keep foreign elements (pdf.js `"12R"` ids, no id, other kinds) verbatim and re-post them; contract rects and our quads are both PDF user space (rotation/CropBox only affect display); Lean `do` rejects `← x` inside an `if … then … else` argument — bind first (10.2).
* `DisplayItem`/`ImageSource` have no `DecidableEq` (tests need a fuelled hand-written equality); inside `match h : …` building a proof from `h`, `rw [hd]` fails with "motive is not type correct" — `simp [hd]` works; a cache changes behaviour if the source's `fetch` depends on state that `decode` advances (not the case for `docSource`) (7.9b).
* Multi-line `{ x with … }` continuation lines must reach the first field's column; `(← st.get)` is not allowed inside a `fun` within `do` — bind first; to test that a highlight is gone count tinted pixels over the box (a centre pixel often lands on black glyph ink, which multiply keeps black) (9.5g).
* Inside `namespace SafePdf.Tests`, `.tooLarge` resolves against `SafePdf.Error` — spell out the type; `String.drop` returns a slice in v4.36; the adversarial `xobject-fanout.pdf` interpret bound (2 s) was exceeded once (2.9 s) under heavy machine load — a timing flake to watch (9.5d).
* bib-lean's PUT makes the new file the one later GETs serve (`storage/KEY/NAME 2.pdf`), so a conformance self-test must not re-add marks already present or S12 (stable keys) fails; grep for our `/NM`/`/Contents` must also look for the hex form (10.4).
* Compiled `Int` above 2³¹ is a bignum: `sqrtQ` radicands reach 2⁷⁸–2⁹³ for real pens; `sqrtQ` precision depends on the reduced denominator (normalise with an exact gcd); `if_pos/if_neg/dif_neg` deprecated → `simp only [h, ↓reduceIte]`; `perf` call graphs are broken here (no frame pointers) — `perf report --children -g none` gives inclusive percentages (7.9c).
* `swap` is Mathlib/Batteries — use `rotate_left`; `openDoc_ok` lives in `Spec.PageTree`; `split at h` on `containerLength` also splits the goal's identical match; ByteArray equality from `.data` equality: `cases a; cases b; simp only at h; rw [h]` (9.5e).
* Lean code written through a Python non-raw `'''…'''` string loses `\`+newline continuations (silently changes `s!` strings and Tcl line ends) — write such edits from a file or use raw strings (9.11).
* Driving the viewer (8.5): generated key events go to the X focus — `focus -force W; update` first under load; in continuous mode the status line is overwritten by deferred renders (read it in the same `send`); `event generate -time` must fit 32 bits; outside smoke mode the context menu is posted with a grab (unpost before invoking); search hit counts are per page.
* `TextView.adversarialFailures` hard-codes the number of files in `Tests/adversarial/encrypted/` (now 15) (9.5e2).
* After `close(3)` the runtime may reopen fd 3 (e.g. `/dev/null`) — test "not `pipe:`", not "absent"; close fd 3 only after a valid token was read from it; under `set -e`, `cmd; check $?` aborts at the first failing check (10.1b).
* A DCT image grey on some pages usually means its full-size decode hit `maxDecodedStreamBytes` (MuPDF never decodes at full size); libjpeg decodes subsampled chroma with a larger IDCT at reduced scales (4:2:0 needs no upsampling there; fancy upsampling off at 1/8); `mutool show` needs `-be` for raw stream bytes; measure memory with Python `resource` (7.10).
* Tk: `rename send {}` unregisters the app but `tk appname NAME` re-registers it; X resources are applied as widget defaults (incl. `*takeFocus`, evaluated by keyboard traversal) and `option clear` reloads them — override at top priority; the Lean runtime waits for dedicated tasks at exit (use `IO.Process.exit`); time-to-display with async rendering includes queueing (8.7/8.8).
* `scripts/viewer-corpus.sh` can miss the last page's display once in a full run (arxiv-1409.0473 p15 on eb78544; 15/15 on 3 re-runs) — the driver should wait for the final render before moving on (8.5 follow-up).
* `ByteArray.mk (Array.replicate n v)` builds a boxed array first (8 bytes per byte): a transient 8× peak — push into a pre-sized buffer (`fillBytes`); averaging 2^k blocks while decoding and then box-filtering at placement blurs measurably — filter once, to the placement's target (8.6).
* Returning from a Lean `main` joins every dedicated `IO.asTask` task (`lean_finalize_task_manager`) — any path that can leave a render running must end with `IO.Process.exit`; a background `lake build` started before an edit can pick up the edited file, so a copied "old" binary may be the new one (8.9).
* Tk: generated key events to a just-created widget need `update` first; two quick press/release pairs make `<Double-Button-1>` (a draw tool must start a stroke on it too); bubble commands run at `after idle` — `update` before checking; `\` continuation inside `s!` interpolation is a parse error (9.9).
* An optional parameter on a pattern-matching def: `(stages) (b) (cap : Nat := 0) := match stages, b with …`; after `unfold inflateAt` proofs see `emptyWithCapacity 0` — `simp only [ByteArray.emptyWithCapacity_eq_empty]` (8.10).
* pdf.js in Node draws a FreeText with non-embedded `/Helv` only when `standardFontDataUrl` points at pdfjs-dist's `standard_fonts/`; pdf.js 4.10 does not expose `/NM`; ImageMagick writes 16-bit PNGs from labelled composites — pass `-depth 8`; tool pop-overs need `update` after the tool-button click (9.10).
* `pkill -x Xvfb` / `pkill -f <script>` kill other agents' displays or your own shell — kill only PIDs you started; use `xvfb-run -a`, never fixed display numbers (9.r1, 9.r2).
* Tk 9 trackpad input is testable on X11: `event generate .c <TouchpadScroll> -delta [expr {dx*65536 + (dy & 0xffff)}]`; a trackpad gesture carries both axes, so wheel state is per axis (9.r1).
* Tk `moveto` fractions need more than 6 digits for documents taller than ~500,000 px (9.r1).
* `send` from wish blocks while the viewer sits in a dialog's `vwait`: use `send -async`; `xwininfo -root -children` finds the dialog; `xdotool key Escape` reaches a focused Tk dialog without a WM (9.r2).
* A rename replaces a read-only file in a writable directory: check `file writable` before overwriting (9.r2).
* On X11 Tk reads `Command` as Meta: add `<Command-…>` bindings only on aqua; `namespace eval ::tk::mac {}` before defining `::tk::mac::Quit` off aqua (9.r3).
* xprop shows `_NET_WM_ICON` only with `-f _NET_WM_ICON 32c`; without a WM the icon property sits on the wrapper (X parent of `winfo id .`) (9.r3).
* Our renderer leaves non-embedded standard-14 text unpainted without substitute fonts — on a stock Mac (no URW/TeX Gyre) that means invisible text (9.r5 → 9.r7).
* ImageMagick `import` writes 16-bit PPM unless given `-depth 8`; pixel checks on the page photo cannot see canvas items — screenshot instead (9.r5).
* Sample screen x (`winfo rootx .c` + offset), not canvas x, to see layout flicker; Tk runs a `<Configure>` binding during geometry layout, before repainting — relayout there to avoid an intermediate frame, but only for width changes (a find bar inside the window replaces status lines smoke checks read) (9.r4).
* Tk's X11 save dialog appends `-defaultextension` only to names without a dot; add the extension yourself (9.r6).
* Tk 9 aqua `tk_getSaveFile` puts a 340 pt Format pop-up in a 300 pt accessory view whenever `-filetypes` is given: omit `-filetypes` on aqua for save dialogs (open dialogs' 240 pt pop-up fits) (9.r6).
* A double quote inside a Tcl comment ends the Lean string literal holding the script; `expr` cannot compare a bare `$w.f.b` — use `string equal` (9.r6).
* `ulimit -f` cannot simulate a full disk (SIGXFSZ kills the process); `sudo mount -t tmpfs -o size=8k` gives a 16 KiB filesystem (9.r6).
* A smoke step that reaches a real `tk_getSaveFile` hangs until the 60 s timeout — every dialog needs a smoke answer (9.r6).
* A cmap format 4 `idDelta` adds to the character code (cannot map a range to one glyph — use `idRangeOffset` or format 6); `System.FilePath.mk "/" / "usr"` gives `//usr` (9.r7).
* Worktree agents: the sandbox guard refuses compound commands with pipes/heredocs for edits — write scripts to the scratchpad and run them (9.r4, 9.r7).
* Substitute fonts (7.7, 9.r7): search order and diagnostics in `docs/raster.md`; `SAFEPDF_SUBST_FONTS=report` prints the file chosen per standard-14 face, `=0` disables substitutes.
* Plain `destroy .note` does not run its `WM_DELETE_WINDOW` handler; smoke steps that destroy a popup directly bypass close rules (9.r12).
* A `::safepdf::emit` from Tcl while Lean is inside `run` re-enters the Lean handler synchronously (works; 9.r12 relies on it); the window title refreshes only in `syncMenus` — call it in smoke steps that change the store without an event (9.r12).
* In a multi-line `{ s with a := …,` the following field lines must align with the first, else "unexpected identifier; expected '}'" (9.r8).
* hyperref writes `/PageMode /UseOutlines /OpenAction [p1 /Fit]` by default: with 9.r8 most LaTeX papers open at Page Fit with the outline (9.r8).
* `Spec/Content.lean` refers to `Interp.simple.match_32` by number: a new `match` before `simple` in `Interp.lean` renumbers it — write helpers there without `match` (9.r11a).
* `FormCache.env` rebuilds `Env` field by field: a new `Env` field must be copied there or it silently resets (9.r11a).
* `exact Thm ..` fills a theorem's optional argument with its default — give theorem parameters no defaults (9.r11a).
* `Core.Doc.Entry` already exists (page-tree walk); `ttk::treeview -values` takes a Tcl list — set cells with `.tv set ITEM COL VALUE` (9.r10).
* Read a canvas's size from `winfo height`/`ismapped`, not `yview` (before mapping `yview` reports everything visible, defeating lazy rendering); the viewer's Tk interp for `send` is `tk*`; renaming a menu label breaks `entryconfigure <label>` in `platformScript` (9.r9).
* Merging `docs/viewer.md` / `scripts/viewer-smoke.sh` across parallel tasks: take one side and re-apply the other task's additions as a scripted patch (9.r10).
* A Tk text-widget tree whose rows start closed must hide them with the `-elide` tag at fill time — the model's closed state alone draws a closed triangle over visible rows (9.r11b).
* `ttk::checkbutton` has no `-wraplength`: embed it and put the name as text after it; test a packed sidebar button with `winfo manager`, not `winfo ismapped` (unmapped while the sidebar is closed) (9.r11b).
* Tk Aqua (10.11): `tclsh9.1` + `package require Tk` exits silently — use `wish9.1` and write results to a file; generated clicks without `-time` count as double clicks even 700 ms apart; `wm attributes -fullscreen 0` in the same turn as `-fullscreen 1` is ignored; `focus` is empty whenever the app is not active (another app in front, locked screen); `send` works on Aqua and `winfo interps` lists every Tk interp of the user.
* macOS shell (10.11): BSD `cmp -n N` reports EOF when the first file ends at N, no `stat -c` (use the script's `file_size`/`file_mode`/`cmp_prefix`); bash 3.2 errors on `"${arr[@]}"` of an empty array under `set -u` (`wrap=(env)` or `${a[@]+"${a[@]}"}`); System Events (`osascript`) needs an Automation permission prompt — watch the front app with `lsappinfo` instead.
* Smoke on the human's Mac (10.11): never run the viewer in the foreground while the human works — the default is background mode (activation policy Prohibited, `-alpha 0`, off screen); a foreground run needs `SAFEPDF_SMOKE_FOREGROUND=1` and the human's go-ahead. Subagents must not kill processes broadly: the human may have their own `viewer` open.
* `#guard let x := e`: a newline after `e` starts the let body whatever its indentation — keep `e` on one line or move it to a `private def` (10.7).
* Tk: a binding on the tag `all` runs after the widget's own binding — a click-away must not close the transient owned by the button being clicked (its release would toggle it back); `winfo ismapped` stays 0 right after `pack` until an update — test with `winfo manager` (10.6).
* `Tcl.quote` wraps `Command-[` in braces (guards must match `Tcl.quote "Command-["`); `\[` is an invalid Lean string escape; a def must come after the defs it uses (10.9).
* Worktree agents share the scratchpad — use task-numbered file names; the worktree guard refuses commands mixing git with paths containing "git" (`gitprojects`) — `cd` and use relative paths (10.6, 10.7, 10.9).
* Worktree isolation can start an agent's worktree at `main` instead of the phase branch — every wt brief must say: check HEAD and fast-forward/reset to the phase branch tip before working (wave 56: all four started at ea31023).
* Smoke on Aqua (10.14): an untimed generated click can count as a double click and select the word under it — give every click a distinct `-time` ≥ 2 s after the previous one; take window positions from the current view after a Save/reopen; deleting a highlight removes a note anchored to its exact quads — check a note's anchor is a point.
* Background smoke mode is not leak-free yet (10.16): the viewer became the front app in the `tint` and `edit` cases; run only the cases you need, always under an `lsappinfo front` watcher (0.5 s) with the kill-own-processes abort rule.
* Tk 9.1 photo `put` takes `#RRGGBBAA`, `get x y -withalpha` returns the alpha; a canvas image item has 2 coords — use `bbox` (10.10).
* macOS substitute faces are Apple's system fonts unless URW is installed (Apple's Courier is heavier than Nimbus Mono PS); to see MuPDF with a given face, embed it via `mutool create` (`%%Font F /path/face.ttc`); MuPDF never embeds its built-in base-14 fonts (10.15).
* v4.36 core lacks `Rat.div_le_div_of_nonneg_right` — `Rat.div_def` + `Rat.mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right`; `decide` can't prove `0 ≤ 64⁻¹` (use `Rat.inv_pos`); first-match proofs: `List.find?_eq_some_iff_append` + `List.pairwise_append` (10.12).
* Tk 9.1 Aqua (10.16): `Tk_Init` sets the Regular activation policy and activates the app itself — setting Prohibited before/after is not enough, override the methods before `Tk_Init`; `wm attributes -fullscreen` uses native `toggleFullScreen:` (a new Space) even for an invisible inactive app once the screen is unlocked; `lsappinfo list` shows each app's type (BackgroundOnly/Foreground) and catches policy leaks even on a locked screen.
* `(cmd | tail)` inside an `&&` chain hides cmd's exit status — check `lake exe tests` on its own; adding a `Cmd` changes the `Cmd.accel` guard and the menubar/`index end` smoke expectations (10.5, 10.8).
* macOS host lacks `qpdf` and ImageMagick `import`: the smoke skips those checks with a printed line — not failures.
* Tk Aqua `send` is never delivered while the screen is locked, even wish→wish; a sync `send` then blocks until the target exits and reports "target application died" — use `send -async` + a file marker with a bounded poll; never `send` to every interp of `winfo interps` on Aqua (stale and other agents' interps are listed) — give a driven viewer a unique appname (10.19). A locked screen also blinds the front-app watcher (always loginwindow) — use `lsappinfo list` types (BackgroundOnly/Foreground) to catch leaks then.
* X11 `send -async` stays in Xlib's output buffer until the sender enters the event loop; a poll with `after 50` never does, so the send never arrives — call `update idletasks` after it (smoke 1l, fixed 2026-11-24). In the Linux sandbox `$DISPLAY=:99` may name a stale socket with no Xvfb behind it: run the smoke with `env -u DISPLAY` so it uses `xvfb-run`.
* After `destroy .`, `winfo` stays defined but throws "application has been destroyed" — test for a widget with `info commands` (10.20).
* A structure field with a default still has to be given in the anonymous constructor `⟨…⟩` — add fields where positional builders won't break (BibLean builds `Cell` positionally); `grind` proves `Rat` min/max/midpoint goals; round adjacent translucent boxes' edges to the nearest pixel (floor/ceil overlaps 1 px) (10.18).
* Lean v4.36 `@[export]` rejects `@&` parameters — exports consume their arguments (`lean_inc` before the call); the module initialiser is `initialize_safe_x2dpdf_<Module>(uint8_t builtin)`; a static `lean_lib` archives only its own modules — use the module's `linkInfoNoExport` + `buildStaticLib` for the closure; system-clang links need `-lcadical`, and no `-mmacosx-version-min` (11.1).
* A docstring on `variable` is a parse error; a local `let rc4 := …` clashes with `Crypt.Method.rc4` under `open SafePdf.Core` (rename) (12.4). `IO.getRandomBytes` (libuv `uv_random`) is documented as not guaranteed cryptographically secure — the viewer also reads `/dev/urandom`.
* v4.36 mergeSort stability: `List.sublist_mergeSort` (+ transitivity/totality of `le`) and `List.pairwise_mergeSort` — no `mergeSort_stable`; `Nat.le` has no `.trans` (use `Nat.le_trans`); `cases` on `some y = some (y.1, g, off)` fails — destructure `y` first (12.7).
* lean4lean (12.6): no release tags — pin a commit whose toolchain matches; its multi-module mode crashes (SIGSEGV) now and then — one process per module; default fuel too low for our kernel terms (raise it); module names match by prefix, so a parent module is checked with its submodules.
* `IO.FS.Handle.read` stays at EOF once it has seen it — bytes appended after a short read may be missed; a recursive `Error.message` with a nested pattern on `SafePdf.Error` fails structural recursion — split the inner match (12.1).
* `Tr` (text render mode) is text state and survives `ET` (12.5); `#eval` captures stdout — interpreted runners write to a file; `import` must precede the `/-! -/` doc in a `lake env lean` script; no `String.trim` in v4.36 (12.6).
* `rw [f]` fails on a well-founded def with a variable fuel — `cases` the fuel or `f.eq_def`; for nested `match` use `rcases h : discr with …`; count with a small recursive function, not `List.filter`+`decide`; `List.Nodup.length_le_of_subset` exists (12.7a).
* `Child.tryWait` reaps the child — a later `Child.wait` throws ECHILD; keep the exit status yourself; `lake env lean --run` interprets imported code (~6× slower) — measure overheads with a compiled exe (12.2a).
* `fun_induction f …` substitutes only the branch's recursive call — unfold other occurrences with `f.eq_def args`; small `_hit`/`_miss`/`_end` step lemmas keep such proofs short (12.7b). A shared message helper for two error cases turns "agree up to message" proofs into `rfl` (12.7d).
* v4.36 `Rat`: `mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left : a ≤ b → 0 ≤ c → c*a ≤ c*b` (pass positionally); missing `div_le_iff`, `num_div_den`, `natCast_one`, `Nat.pos_pow_of_pos` — use `lt_div_iff`/`div_lt_iff` + `not_lt`, `num_divInt_den` + `divInt_eq_div`, `Nat.pow_pos`, `floor_le`/`lt_floor_add_one`; `grind` chokes on casts of `10 ^ k` — `generalize ((10^k : Nat) : Rat) = A at *` first; `decide` can't prove `0 ≤ (1/2000000 : Rat)` — `grind` (12.8).
* Registries: subagent edits of `Tests/Main.lean` are denied — subagents report lines, the orchestrator adds them; a doc comment before a new lakefile target must not split another target's doc comment from its declaration.
* Lean 4.36 on macOS: `IO.Process.spawn`/`output` with `inheritEnv := false` and a non-empty `env` crashes the child (SIGSEGV, exit 139) — an empty `env` works; pass test settings as flags (12.2b/c).
* Under seatbelt `(deny default)` Lean's `IO.Process.output` fails with "operation not permitted" and `IO.asTask .dedicated` still works; extern C helpers must live in modules imported only by the executables that link the C object (12.2b).
* `lake build viewer` alone does not build `worker` — the smoke runs `lake build viewer worker`; with one worker per document a queued neighbour keeps its low priority unless raised (12.2c).
* `Repair` builds no compressed entries — objects only in object streams are null after repair; strict rejects data after `%%EOF` (put a stale trailer between the trailer and `startxref`); a wrong `/ID` fails `withCrypt` at the `/U` check; `List.Nodup.of_map` is missing in v4.36 (`List.pairwise_map` + `.imp`); hand-written xref streams need `/W [1 2 2]` for `free 0 65535` (12.7c).
* v4.36: missing `Rat.div_nonneg`, `Rat.mul_self_nonneg`, `Int.mul_self_nonneg`, `Rat.lt_or_eq_of_le`, `Nat.sqrt_pos`; `if_pos`/`if_neg` deprecated (`simp only [h, ↓reduceIte]`); `Lean.Grind.Semiring.pow_add` works for `Rat`; `clear` Nat facts before a final `grind` (12.8b).
* With the human's screen unlocked, smoke 1l's drive control passes on Aqua (`sends-ok: 1`) — its skip only triggers on locked sessions.
* `lsappinfo list | grep -A8 PATH` spills into the next app's entry — match whole entry blocks; a structure instance broken across lines inside `#guard match …` fails to parse — bind it with a `def`; `Protocol.Limits.ofList?` lists the `Limits` fields by position — adding a field needs `toList` and `ofList?` updated (12.2d).
* R6 does not help encrypted files (containers are encrypted, decryption exists only after repair); the repair view's diff does not count compressed entries present on both sides; `rw [lemma]` then `rw [hr] at h` leaves the goal unrewritten (12.7e).
* CI (Ubuntu) has no substitute fonts in the worker's font directories — worker renders are blank there; the seccomp-confined worker passes `worker` and `worker-confinement` on CI (first Linux run, b0f128e); `c/worker_sandbox.c` line 128 triggers a harmless `-Wmisleading-indentation` warning.
* macOS 26: `setrlimit(RLIMIT_AS)`/`setrlimit(RLIMIT_DATA)` fail with EINVAL, `memorystatus_control` → EPERM; `task_info` (phys_footprint) and `setrlimit(RLIMIT_CPU)` work under `(deny default)`; on Linux the seccomp list allows `prlimit64` but not `setrlimit` — use `syscall(__NR_prlimit64)`; `until` is a Lean keyword (12.3).
* `parseIndirectResolved` has an optional `pre` argument — state function equalities as `@parseIndirectResolved l1 = @parseIndirectResolved l2`; `rw` cannot reach a call inside a match branch that binds its arguments — rewrite with a `funext` whole-function equality (12.7f).
* Workflow edits can be pushed since 2026-11-24 (the token has the `workflow` scope) — CI changes go straight into `.github/workflows/ci.yml`.
* Ubuntu 24.04's `tk-dev` is Tk 8.6 (no `#RRGGBBAA` photo colours, no `get -withalpha`, no `<TouchpadScroll>`); a generated event of an unknown type is swallowed by `sendEvent`, so a missing event type shows up as a hang; Tk runs received `send`s only on a "secure" X server and `-async` hides the refusal (10.c1).
* Lean 4.36's runtime is mimalloc with eager arena commit on Linux — absolute `RLIMIT_DATA`/`RLIMIT_AS` are meaningless for Lean processes; a SIGXCPU/SIGABRT core dump to a pipe `core_pattern` can hold a dying process for many seconds (`RLIMIT_CORE` 0, `PR_SET_DUMPABLE` 0); flush a child's stdout per line or lose it on a signal (12.c1).
* lean4lean's driver matches modules by prefix and replays on task threads (8 MiB stacks): parents with submodules segfaulted on Linux — use an exact-module driver on the main thread with a raised stack (12.c2).
* macOS RSS is sticky (freed Tk/Lean buffers mostly not returned): measure scenario peaks, not pool totals; a displayed Tk 9 photo costs ~10.5 B/px on macOS; `tk scaling` 1.334 makes 600 % of Letter 31 Mpx (12.3b).
* A push cancels the running CI on that branch (`cancel-in-progress`); batch commits locally while CI runs.
* C API v2 (11.3a1–a3): three headers must compile together (Swift imports them as one module) — shared `safepdf_box` (doubles) lives in `safepdf_core.h`, `safepdf_marks.h` includes it; marks' buffer is `safepdf_marks_bytes`/`safepdf_free_marks_bytes` (frees data only; core's `safepdf_free_bytes` frees the struct too); view's rational types are `safepdf_rat_box`/`safepdf_rat_point`/`safepdf_view_action`; `lakefile.lean` `ffiModules` lists every FFI root (append new ones there); run the four-header compile check after adding any type.
* Lean 4.36 C exports: `Int64` → `uint64_t`, `Bool` → `uint8_t`, `Unit` argument → `lean_box(0)`; `IO` exports take no world argument but return `lean_io_result_*`; `Except` ctor 0 = error, 1 = ok; scalars inside `Prod` are boxed; `Int.toInt64` wraps silently — range-check first; core has no `Float.toRatParts` (use `Float.frExp`); a second module initialiser after `lean_io_mark_end_initialization` works; `seal` is a keyword (11.3a2/a3).
* Worker spawn (11.3a1): `Session` spawns with cwd `/`, so a relative worker path passes `pathExists` but exec fails — make it absolute; a failed exec flushes the parent's unflushed stdio into the response pipe ("answer of 1178683724 bytes" = "FAIL") — flush before spawning.
* Lean syntax (11.3a1/a2): a doc comment before `#guard` is a parse error (use `--`); `enc (xs : List Nat).map f` parses as `.map` on `enc`'s result; inside a namespace ending in `Doc`, write `Core.Doc.*` explicitly.
* `macos/selftest.sh` fails while the human has a `safepdf-mac` open (its Foreground check matches by name) — not a regression (11.3a1; fix → 11.3a5).
* FfiSelect (11.3a4): inside namespace `SafePdf.Shell`, `Select.nearest` does not resolve while an export is itself named `nearest` — write `App.Select.nearest`; doubles from `safepdf_page_index` go back to exact rationals through `FfiMarks.ratOfFloat`, so C containment checks agree with Lean.
* Agent worktrees are cut from `main`, not the phase branch: when `main` has moved past the base, `merge --ff-only <base>` fails — the brief's HEAD check should say `git reset --hard <base>` on a clean fresh worktree (11.3a4).
* Swift shell (11.3b): `swiftc -O` finds Swift 6 "sending risks data race" errors that `-typecheck` misses — pass C pointers into `MainActor.assumeIsolated` as `UInt(bitPattern:)`; `NSWindowController.document` exists (don't name a property `document`); `viewDidEndLiveResize` is NSView's — use `windowDidEndLiveResize`; the build needs Swift 6 and a macOS 26 SDK (26-only calls gated by `#if compiler(>=6.2)` + `#available`).
* Lean 4.36's bundled `libgmp.a`/`libuv.a` are built for macOS 15.0 (318 link warnings, kept in `macos/build/swift-link.log`) and Lake's `worker` has `minos 99.0` — the app cannot really target macOS 14 without rebuilding them (11.3b; question for the human).
* `run_all.sh` collects the four shim objects from the test scripts' output dirs (`build/safepdf_core.o`, `build/tests/safepdf_view.o`, `build/marks-test/…`, `build/select-test/…`) — keep `build.sh` producing `build/safepdf_core.o` (11.3a5).
* macOS app (11.3c1): fit zooms must use a canvas size that does not change when scrollers appear (legacy scrollers, as in the self-test's off-screen scroll view, otherwise make the fit overflow); anchoring checks only mean something when the scroll was not clamped at an edge (`lastScrollClamped`) — small pages always clamp; Swift `Int64(Double)` traps on non-finite or huge values — clamp first.
* macOS app (11.3c2): a new layout moves the clip view and fires `boundsDidChange` before the viewer's own scroll — hold the current-page rule during programmatic layout/scroll; Swift 6: a struct's stored default cannot call a `@MainActor` enum (nor `==` on it) — use the C literal; AppKit 26.1's scroll-edge style imports as `NSScrollEdgeEffectStyle.soft`; `CGEvent(scrollWheelEvent2Source:units:.pixel)` gives precise scroll NSEvents usable headless; no golden has `/PageLayout` or `/OpenAction` — the self-test generates the Tk smoke 1s fixtures.
* macOS app (11.3d1/d2): anonymous C enums (`SAFEPDF_MOD_*`) import as Int constants — write `UInt32(X)`, not `.rawValue`; the search field's delegate must declare `NSSearchFieldDelegate`; menu commands reach the window controller while a text field has focus — send ⌘C/⌘A to the field editor, and keep (hidden) Cut/Paste items or fields lose ⌘X/⌘V; a Lean namespace named after an imported module shadows it (`Ffi.Sidebar` → `Sidebar.onLoad` resolved to itself — hence `Ffi.SidebarApi`); `/NonFullScreenPageMode` lives in `/ViewerPreferences`; layers changes must also drop the text indexes and pass the layers state to `safepdf_doc_page_index` (wired at integration, as Tk's `applyLayers`).
* macOS marks (11.3e1): `Store.isComment` needs non-empty text (a fresh comment counts only once typed); rotated pages (tree.pdf) — move/position tests must go through `PageTransform`; give `selftest.sh` an absolute out dir (relative breaks the identity-line check).
* macOS marks store (11.3e2): its Lean objects are not thread-shared — any work on the store outside its lock (e.g. building `Save.preview` on a background queue) must `lean_mark_mt` the handle under the lock first, or reference counts race and the app crashes in `lean_alloc_small_object`; `NSRect.intersection` returns a null rect (infinite origin) when the rects don't meet — `Int(...)` on it traps; typed `throws(E)` is not inferred inside closures; `NSAlert` shows its first button rightmost.
* macOS smoke (11.5): ⌘H is the application menu's Hide — the table's Highlight is ⌃⌘H; ⌘G is Find next, Go to page is ⌥⌘G; overlapping page links: the topmost wins, so pick a point only the target link covers; Add note while a highlight is selected makes a comment (mutool lists a Popup); headless NSScrollView does not scroll without a window — the session moves the clip view; `NSWindow.close()` is a no-op on a window never ordered in (post `willClose` yourself); never edit a bash script while it runs; `script` is a Lake keyword (11.4).

* Sharing across a call, found in the wild (2026-11-25; see "Lean 4 in-place updates" above): `Run.stepR` read `m.oc` *after* `localStep … m …`, so the machine stayed shared, the callee's destructuring no longer owned the path array and every `Array.push` copied it — 100,000 `l` operators took 7.1 s (9.r11a added the read; no test noticed). Bind projections before the call that consumes the structure. To confirm without a build, paste the definition under a new name into a scratch file with `set_option trace.compiler.ir.result true in`, run `lake env lean` on it and look for `inc` of the structure just before the call; in stack samples the symptom is `lean_copy_expand_array` under the fold.
* Numeric literals and powers in compiled code (2026-11-25): a `Nat`/`Int` literal ≥ 2³² inside a function body is emitted as `lean_cstr_to_nat("…")` — a decimal parse through GMP on every call — unless it ends up in an `_init_` closed term, and a named `def` constant may still be inlined at its use sites (`Stroke.bound44`); `2 ^ n` with a run-time exponent calls `lean_nat_pow`, which goes through GMP even for small results; `Int` is unboxed only below 2³¹ in absolute value (`Nat` up to 2⁶³ − 1), so products of two 10⁶-scaled `Fixed.raw` are heap bignums. Check the generated C under `.lake/build/ir/` for `lean_cstr_to_nat(` outside `_init_`.
* Profiling in the Linux sandbox (2026-11-25): `perf` fails (`libslang.so.2: undefined symbol: tgetflag`). Sample with `gdb -p PID -batch -ex "thread apply all bt 12"` in a loop — all threads, because Lean runs `main` on a separate thread and the main thread only shows the wait; project symbols are `lp_safe_x2dpdf_…`.
* What the existing rigs cannot see (2026-11-25): the fuzz smoke caps inputs at 4,096 bytes and drives parser entry points only; `adversarial` had no long-structure file; release builds pass `-DNDEBUG`, so `lean.h`'s `assert(i < lean_array_size(o))` behind proof-carrying indexing is compiled out (Lake's `debug` build type is `-O0 -g` without it); `scripts/hygiene.py` matches source text, so a `partial` generated by `deriving Repr` on a nested inductive, an `@[extern]` or an `axiom` passes it.
* The sandbox checkout can be far behind `origin` (2026-11-25: 143 commits, two whole phases — work is pushed from the human's Mac): `git fetch` and compare with `origin/main` before measuring, reading the ledger or planning.
* 2026-11-28 (13.1) — A value read after a call keeps it shared: `Run.stepR` read `m.oc` after `localStep env fr m s op`, so every `addSegs` push copied the path array; bind the field before the call (instance 4.12 in `docs/proof-patterns.md`). IR check (`set_option trace.compiler.ir.result true`) on `gsR`, the `Do` branch and `localStep`'s branches was not run — source reading only; do it when touching them.
* 2026-11-28 (13.2) — `Std.TreeSet (List UInt8) compare` gives an ordered key set with `TreeSet.contains_insert`; the `TransOrd`/`LawfulEqOrd` instances for `List UInt8` already exist, so no hand-written ordering proof. `Content/Ops.lean`'s `expandInline` had a second per-key `find?` quadratic besides `inlineDict` — fix both when touching inline dictionaries.
* 2026-11-28 (13.2) — Duplicate-dictionary-key error positions are unchanged by the key-set change (40k-key file: byte 537828); `parseDict`, `inlineDict` and `DictAgreeN` now carry the `seen` set (`SeenOf seen acc`).
* 2026-11-28 (13.8) — Derived `Repr` on nested inductives generates `partial`; a hand-written instance must copy `nest (if prec >= 1024 then 1 else 2)` and parenthesise nullary constructors at 1024 to keep the output byte-identical. Plain (non-nested) `deriving Repr` is total and fine.
* 2026-11-28 (13.17) — `scripts/profile.sh` (`-r` reruns a short command so ≥500 samples are reachable) and `scripts/profile_agg.py`; gdb cannot unwind Lean frames deeply (~3.5% of bignum samples have no project frame), thread 1 is idle in `__syscall_cancel_arch`, and wall times taken while profiling mean nothing. Timings taken under load: re-measure the phase-start baseline on a quiet machine before M13's speed comparison.
* 2026-11-28 — `lake exe tests` takes over 2 minutes: run it in the background, never in a 120 s tool call. A fresh worktree's first `lake build` is silent for a long time. A stray `cat` in a shell command blocks on stdin.
* 2026-11-28 (13.3) — Scaling suite `Tests/Scaling.lean` (suite `scaling`, excluded from `compiled-vs-interpreted.sh` via `SKIPPED_SUITES`): minimum of three runs, n doubled until one run ≥ 37.5 ms, re-measured over six runs when the ratio exceeds 3. A probe tagged `known := "13.x"` only warns; M13 part (1) needs rows 13.22–13.28 done and every tag removed. Setup that calls a quadratic function stalls calibration, so search probes build their input directly. `link-annots` stays below the floor because `maxAnnots` = 4096. `one-path-segments` is the closest passing probe (2.5× at 16k→32k).
* 2026-11-28 (13.9) — `scripts/envscan.lean` (run `lake env lean --run scripts/envscan.lean MODULE...` after `lake build`): `_unsafe_rec` twins have `DefinitionSafety.partial`, not `unsafe`, so the scan checks `isPartial` too (419 twins, 0 partial); `T.ctorIdx` carries `@[implemented_by T.ctorIdx._impl]`, allowed explicitly; `native_decide` creates a per-theorem axiom (`t._native.native_decide.ax_1_1`) and is caught as a disallowed axiom; `SafePdf.Api.openFile` is the one allowed `IO` in a strict module (`ioAllowed`). Strict list comes from `scripts/hygiene.py modules`; a module the umbrella forgot is still scanned. `scripts/test-hygiene.sh` runs 24 planted-violation fixtures (13 source, 10 environment, 1 clean). Not checked: that removing the `openFile` allowlist entry makes the guard fail.
* 2026-11-28 (13.18) — The compiler inlines arity-0 definitions whose body is a literal and does not lift the literal into a closed term, so a named constant such as `Stroke.bound44` must be `@[noinline]` to read the `_init_` global; `lean_nat_shiftl` also goes through `mpz` in this runtime, so only a `UInt64` shift (`Image.pow2`, `pow2_eq`) removes the GMP call. `scripts/check-codegen.sh` fails on `lean_cstr_to_nat(` outside `_init_` in the generated C of `Core/Raster` and `Core/Content` (CI `build` job). Remaining bignum frames: `Nat.sqrt` in `Stroke.sqrtQ` (needs 13.19's fixed-point approach), per-image `2 ^ x` in `lutComp`, `Norm.defaultRange`, `ImgDict.reduce`, `fitScale`, `fitTarget`, `l2Factor.go`, and `Quarantine.CCITT`, `Content.Fixed.ofDecimal`, `Type3.weightsAtI`.
* 2026-11-28 — A fresh worktree's first `lake build` can fail fetching the `jpeg2000` package (HTTP 502): `rm -rf .lake` and retry. Subagents die on API connection errors: resume them with SendMessage; their worktree survives.
* 2026-11-28 (13.6) — `scripts/fuzz.sh long` (stops starting iterations after 240 s, `FUZZ_LONG_SECONDS`; CI step "Fuzz long inputs", 10-minute timeout) runs `--long`: 28 repetition recipes, up to 4 MiB, linear budget 1000 ms + rate per KiB (`parse` 1500, `parseObjectAt`/`parseIndirectAt`/`lexAll` 1000, `runBytes` 25000 µs/KiB; worst measured 264 / 169 / 61 / 98 / 7642, margins 3.3–16×), exit 3 on a budget failure. On `abfb5b9` it finds 13.2 (`dict-keys`, 4.6 s budget vs 9.3 s) and 13.1 (`--recipe path-lines`, 500k segments abandoned after 148 s); on the fixed tree 0 failures in 353 iterations. `Core.Content.runBytes` was added as a 7th entry point because the parser entries cannot reach 13.1; 13.7 still owns the full interpreter target. Sandbox: Lean stdout to a file is block-buffered (wait on an `exit=` marker), the wall clock jumps (use the fuzzer's `elapsed-ms`), and compound shell commands are refused inside subagent worktrees.
* 2026-11-28 (13.22) — Flat `/Kids` was quadratic because `popLive`/`popLiveB` tested `e.num ∈ visited` on a `List Nat`; `Core/Doc.lean` now carries `VSet visited` (a `Std.TreeSet Nat compare` provably mirroring the list, `VSet.has_iff`) in `WalkState.seen`, the list stays as the specification. `if seen.has x then …` on a `Bool` makes `by_cases` negatives rewrite to `= false`: add `Bool.false_eq_true` to the `simp only` set.
* 2026-11-28 (13.23) — Two quadratics in the revision chain: the `off ∈ visited` / `visited.length` per round in `Xref/Chain.lean` (now `Seen visited`: tree set plus stored length, argument `sn` threaded through `step`/`walk`/`stepB`/`walkB`) and `mergeChain` as a right fold of `mergeTwo` (now `mergeRounds`, log₂ n linear passes; `findSome?_mergePairs`, `lookupEntry_mergeRounds` in `Spec/XrefTerm.lean`). `lake env lean` on a dependent file gives misleading arity errors until `lake build SafePdf.Spec.XrefTerm` has rebuilt its `.olean`. 64k revisions open in 0.14 s (8.2 s for 32k before).
* 2026-11-28 (13.4a) — Counted steps are authoritative; replayed pixels are a lower bound on 53 of 150 transparency-group pages (cause not found). A single fill can cost up to `h·(S·E+w+1)` (a million slivers ≈ 6.5G steps). Strokes are charged only their composite; the transparency driver counts no steps.
